### [1] [SWE-QA: A Dataset and Benchmark for Complex Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24814)
*Laïla Elkoussy,Julien Perez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-QA是一个包含9,072道多选题的文本与代码语料库，用于基准测试多跳代码理解能力。它从12个Python仓库中系统生成，评估声明与调用、交互实体等推理模式。对15个语言模型（360M-671B参数）的评估显示最佳准确率仅74.41%，密集架构比混合专家模型高出10-14个百分点，推理增强变体的收益不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码理解基准仅聚焦于孤立的代码片段，而实际软件开发中开发者需要跨多个分散的代码段连接信息进行复杂推理，存在评估任务与真实需求之间的差距。

Method: 基于解析的实体提取和大语言模型辅助的问题构建，从12个Python仓库系统生成9,072道多选题，包含干扰项验证，覆盖声明与调用、交互实体等推理模式。

Result: 15个语言模型评测中最佳准确率为74.41%，密集架构优于混合专家模型10-14个百分点，推理增强变体效果不一致。

Conclusion: 多跳代码推理对现有模型仍有显著挑战，密集架构表现更稳定，SWE-QA可有效区分真正的理解与浅层模式匹配。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce SWE-QA, a text and code corpus aimed at benchmarking multi-hop code comprehension, addressing the gap between simplified evaluation tasks and the complex reasoning required in real-world software development. While existing code understanding benchmarks focus on isolated snippets, developers must routinely connect information across multiple dispersed code segments. The dataset comprises 9,072 multiple-choice questions systematically generated from 12 Python repositories of SWE-bench, evaluating several recurrent reasoning patterns like Declaration-and-Call questions that link entity definitions to their usage, and Interacting-Entity questions that examine the dynamic relationships among multiple collaborating components. Generated through parsing-based entity extraction and Large Language Model assisted question construction with carefully validated distractors, the benchmark distinguishes genuine comprehension from superficial pattern matching. Evaluation of 15 language models (360M to 671B parameters) reveals significant challenges in multi-hop reasoning, with best performance reaching 74.41% accuracy. Dense architectures consistently outperform mixture-of-experts models by 10-14 percentage points, while reasoning-enhanced variants show inconsistent benefits.

</details>


### [2] [Programming with Data: Test-Driven Data Engineering for Self-Improving LLMs from Raw Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24819)
*Chenkai Pan,Xinglong Xu,Yuhang Xu,Yujun Wu,Siyuan Li,Jintao Chen,Conghui He,Jingxuan Wei,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"用数据编程"（Programming with Data）原则，通过将结构化知识表示作为训练数据和评估的共同基础，使数据工程生命周期与软件开发生命周期精确对应，从而实现模型故障的可追溯诊断和定向修复，在16个学科领域验证了该方法在不同规模和架构模型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在领域微调时缺乏反馈机制：当模型在领域任务上失败时，无法诊断训练数据中具体缺失了什么，只能盲目添加更多数据。

Method: 将源语料中提取的结构化知识表示作为训练数据和评估的共同基础，建立数据工程与软件开发的对应关系：训练数据→源代码，模型训练→编译，基准测试→单元测试，故障驱动的数据修复→调试。模型故障被分解为概念级缺失和推理链断裂，可追溯到具体的数据缺陷并通过定向修补解决。

Result: 在自然科学、工程、生物医学、社会科学等16个学科领域验证了该方法，每次修复周期在不同规模和架构的模型上均产生一致的性能提升，且不损害通用能力。

Conclusion: 训练数据与模型行为之间的关系是可结构追踪和系统修复的，为将人类专业知识可靠地工程化注入语言模型建立了原则性基础。

Abstract: Reliably transferring specialized human knowledge from text into large language models remains a fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence. Fine-tuning on domain corpora has enabled substantial capability gains, but the process operates without feedback: when a model fails on a domain task, there is no method to diagnose what is deficient in the training data, and the only recourse is to add more data indiscriminately. Here we show that when a structured knowledge representation extracted from the source corpus serves as the shared foundation for both training data and evaluation, the complete data-engineering lifecycle maps onto the software development lifecycle in a precise and operative way: training data becomes source code specifying what the model should learn, model training becomes compilation, benchmarking becomes unit testing, and failure-driven data repair becomes debugging. Under this correspondence, model failures decompose into concept-level gaps and reasoning-chain breaks that can be traced back to specific deficiencies in the data and repaired through targeted patches, with each repair cycle producing consistent improvements across model scales and architectures without degrading general capabilities. We formalize this principle as Programming with Data and instantiate it across sixteen disciplines spanning the natural sciences, engineering, biomedicine, and the social sciences, releasing a structured knowledge base, benchmark suite, and training corpus as open resources. By demonstrating that the relationship between training data and model behaviour is structurally traceable and systematically repairable, this work establishes a principled foundation for the reliable engineering of human expertise into language models.

</details>


### [3] [A systematic literature Review for Transformer-based Software Vulnerability detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24822)
*Fiza Naseer,Javed Ali Khan,Muhammad Yaqoob,Alexios Mylonas,Ishaya Gambo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2021-2025年间80项使用Transformer模型识别软件漏洞的研究，将模型分为编码器、解码器和组合架构三类，分析了研究趋势、数据集、编程语言、评估指标等维度，并指出了数据不平衡、可解释性、可扩展性和跨语言泛化等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于机器学习和深度学习漏洞识别方法的系统文献综述，但缺乏以Transformer为中心的专门分析。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统梳理Transformer模型在软件漏洞识别领域的应用现状。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham系统文献综述指南，从研究趋势、数据集与来源、编程语言、Transformer框架、检测详细级别、评估指标、参考模型、漏洞类型和实验配置等多个视角，对80篇发表于2021-2025年间的研究进行了系统性评估。

Result: 将Transformer模型划分为编码器、解码器和组合架构三类，分析了应用于源代码、日志和智能合约的预训练和微调版本。揭示了主流研究趋势、常用基准数据集和基线模型，并识别出数据不平衡、可解释性、可扩展性和跨编程语言泛化等关键技术问题。

Conclusion: 通过整合现有证据并识别未解决的研究空白，本综述为研究人员和从业者开发更可靠、精确且可解释的基于Transformer的漏洞识别系统提供了综合性参考资源。

Abstract: Context: Software vulnerabilities pose significant security threats to software systems, especially as software is increasingly used across many areas of daily life, including health, government, and finance. Recently, transformer-based models have demonstrated promising results in automatic software vulnerability identification due to their robust contextual modelling and representation learning capabilities. Objectives: While numerous systematic literature reviews (SLRs) have examined machine learning and deep learning methods for identifying vulnerabilities, a more transformer-centric analysis remains to be explored. This SLR critically analysed 80 studies published between 2021 and 2025 that utilised transformer models to identify software vulnerabilities. Methods: Using Kitchenhams SLR guidelines, we methodically evaluate current research from various perspectives, encompassing study trends, datasets and sources, programming languages, transformer frameworks, detection detail levels, assessment metrics, reference models, types of vulnerabilities, and experimental configurations. Results: We classify transformer models into encoder, decoder, and combined architectures and analyse both pre-trained and fine-tuned versions utilized on source code, logs, and smart contracts. The results emphasise prevailing research trends, frequently utilised benchmarks, and main baselines. It also uncovers crucial technical issues like data imbalance, interpretability, scalability, and generalization across programming languages. Conclusion: By integrating current evidence and recognising unaddressed research areas, this SLR provides a consolidated resource for researchers and professionals seeking to develop more reliable, precise, and interpretable transformer-based vulnerability identification systems.

</details>


### [4] [FGDM: Reasoning Aware Multi-Agentic Framework for Software Bug Detection using Chain of Thought and Tree of Thought Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24831)
*Srita Padmanabhuni,Bhargavi Karuturi,Jerusha Karen Indupalli,Santhan Reddy Chilla,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流图驱动的多智能体框架（FGDM），利用LLM和思维链/思维树提示，结合FAISS向量数据库，在C和Python程序上实现了优于现有方法的缺陷检测与修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在自动化软件缺陷检测中缺乏对代码的全局理解，在处理大规模互联代码库或复杂模块化程序时性能下降；而LLM在捕捉代码模块间依赖关系方面表现有效，因此作者提出FGDM框架。

Method: FGDM框架由四个顺序运行的智能体组成：将代码转换为流图（Flow Graph）、识别错误片段、生成修复代码。所有智能体使用Chain-of-Thought（COT）和Tree-of-Thoughts（TOT）提示，并集成FAISS向量数据库检索相似历史缺陷及其修复方案。

Result: 在Ansible、Black、FastAPI等10个项目的100个程序上（C和Python）进行实验，FGDM在Levenshtein距离上平均降低24.33（Python）和8.37（C），余弦相似度分别达到0.951和0.974，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FGDM通过流图驱动和多智能体协作，结合COT/TOT提示与FAISS检索，有效提升了跨模块代码缺陷检测与修复的性能。

Abstract: Deep Learning methods are becoming prominent in automated software bug detection; however, they lack the global understanding of the given code. Consequently, their performance tends to degrade, especially when they are applied to large interconnected code bases or complex modular programs. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven to be effective at capturing dependencies among multiple interconnected modules in the codebase. This motivated us to propose the Flow-Graph-Driven Multi-Agent Framework (FGDM), which is composed of four agents that operate in a sequential manner. The framework converts the received code to a flow graph, identifies the erroneous segments, and further generates the repaired code. All the employed agents utilize Chain-of-Thought (COT) and Tree-of-Thoughts (TOT) prompts. Additionally, we also integrated with the FAISS vector database to retrieve similar previous bugs and their repairs. We demonstrated the efficacy of the proposed framework over 100 programs from several projects, including Ansible, Black, FastAPI, Keras, Luigi, Matplotlib, Pandas, Scrapy, SpaCy, and Tornado in both C and Python programs. Our experiments demonstrate that the FGDM outperforms the extant approaches and yielded reductions with a mean of 24.33 and 8.37 in Levenshtein distance and similarities of 0.951 and 0.974 in cosine similarity for Python and C, respectively.

</details>


### [5] [Supporting Belonging in Software Engineering Through Role Models Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25099)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将历史人物榜样系统嵌入日常软件工程教学的低干扰教学法，在不改变学习目标和评估的前提下，通过简短的、与主题对齐的情境化介绍，将多元贡献者定位为软件架构发展的基础性人物，从而实现包容性与技术严谨性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育研究中广泛讨论榜样对学生身份认同和归属感的影响，但如何将榜样可见性系统性地嵌入日常工程教学实践尚缺乏关注。

Method: 采用分析性自我民族志方法，基于多轮课程教学中的反思备忘录和教学材料，迭代优化将历史榜样融入核心技术讲座的教学实践。

Result: 这种结构性嵌入方法是一种低干扰的教学实践，能够将表征与学科实质对齐，将多元贡献者定位为软件架构发展的基础性人物；整合过程经过多学期迭代优化，加强了主题对齐和教学流畅性。

Conclusion: 将历史性表征嵌入技术内容可能是在保持工程教育技术严谨性的同时支持包容性的实用机制。

Abstract: Role models are widely discussed in educational research as influential in students identity development and sense of belonging, yet less attention has been given to how role model visibility can be systematically embedded within everyday engineering instruction. This paper presents an analytic autoethnographic account of integrating historically grounded role models into routine software engineering teaching practice. Drawing on reflective memos and instructional artifacts across multiple course offerings, we characterize how brief, topic aligned contextualizations of pioneers were incorporated into core technical lectures without altering learning objectives or assessments. The findings indicate that this structurally embedded approach functioned as a low disruption pedagogical practice that aligned representation with disciplinary substance, situating diverse contributors as foundational to the development of software architecture. The integration was iterative and refined across semesters to strengthen topic alignment and instructional flow. These results suggest that embedding historically grounded representation within technical content may serve as a practical mechanism for supporting inclusivity while preserving technical rigor in engineering education.

</details>


### [6] [R$^3$-SQL: Ranking Reward and Resampling for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25325)
*Hojae Han,Yeonseok Jeong,Seung-won Hwang,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: R$^3$-SQL是一个Text-to-SQL框架，通过统一奖励进行排序和重采样，解决了现有方法中功能等价SQL评分不一致以及正确SQL缺失时无法恢复的问题，在BIRD-dev上达到75.03的执行准确率，成为新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL排序方法存在两个局限：一是对功能等价的SQL查询评分不一致；二是当正确SQL不在候选池中时，排序无法恢复。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: (1) 将候选SQL按执行结果分组，对组进行排序以保证一致性；(2) 结合组间成对偏好和组内点状效用（最佳组排名和大小）进行评分；(3) 引入智能体重采样机制，判断候选池质量并选择性重新采样。

Result: 在BIRD-dev上达到75.03执行准确率，在已公开模型规模的方法中取得新SOTA，并在五个基准测试中均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: R$^3$-SQL通过统一排序与重采样框架，有效解决了功能等价SQL评分不一致和候选池缺失正确SQL的问题，显著提升了Text-to-SQL性能。

Abstract: Modern Text-to-SQL systems generate multiple candidate SQL queries and rank them to judge a final prediction. However, existing methods face two limitations. First, they often score functionally equivalent SQL queries inconsistently despite identical execution results. Second, ranking cannot recover when the correct SQL is absent from the candidate pool. We propose R$^3$-SQL, a Text-to-SQL framework that addresses both issues through unified reward for ranking and resampling. R$^3$-SQL first groups candidates by execution result and ranks groups for consistency. To score each group, it combines a pairwise preference across groups with a pointwise utility from the best group rank and size, capturing relative preference, consistency, and candidate quality. To improve candidate recall, R$^3$-SQL introduces agentic resampling, which judges the generated candidate pool and selectively resamples when the correct SQL is likely absent. R$^3$-SQL achieves 75.03 execution accuracy on BIRD-dev, a new state of the art among methods using models with disclosed sizes, with consistent gains across five benchmarks.

</details>


### [7] [Commit-Aware Learning-Based Test Case Prioritization for Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25363)
*Lorenzo Abbondante,Gerardo Canfora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向持续集成环境的、基于提交信息的测试用例优先级排序方法，通过融合代码变更结构特征、测试覆盖关系和历史执行行为，显著提升了回归测试中故障检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试用例优先级排序方法过度依赖历史执行数据和覆盖率指标，忽略了代码变更中包含的丰富结构信息，导致在CI流水线中效率不足。

Method: 构建统一预测模型，融合版本控制差异的结构属性、测试覆盖关系及历史执行行为，针对新提交估计每个测试套件发现故障的概率，据此排序执行顺序。

Result: 在五个Defects4J项目上的跨项目验证中，基于提交信息的TCP方法在分类和优先级排序有效性上显著优于非提交感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 引入提交结构语义能显著增强回归故障检测能力，并实现CI环境中鲁棒且可泛化的学习型TCP方法。

Abstract: Regression testing in Continuous Integration (CI) pipelines is increasingly costly due to the growing size and execution frequency of test suites. Test Case Prioritization (TCP) mitigates this problem by reordering tests to expose faults earlier. However, most existing techniques rely primarily on historical execution data and coverage metrics, neglecting the rich structural information contained in code changes. This paper proposes a commit-aware, learning-based TCP method that combines structural properties of version-control diffs, test coverage relations, and historical execution behavior into a unified predictive model. Given a new commit, the method estimates the probability that each test suite will reveal at least one failure and prioritizes test execution accordingly. We evaluate our method on five Defects4J projects using a leave-one-project-out cross-project validation setting. Results show that the commit-aware TCP significantly outperform non-commit-aware-baselines in both classification and prioritization effectiveness. Our findings show that including commit structural semantics substantially enhances regression fault detection and enables robust, generalizable learning-based TCP in CI environments.

</details>


### [8] [CoRE: A Fine-Grained Code Reasoning Benchmark Beyond Output Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25399)
*Jun Gao,Yun Peng,Qian Qiao,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Shiyang Zhang,Shichao Weng,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRE基准，通过实现不变性和过程透明性两个维度评估LLM的代码推理能力。实验发现：模型在不同等价实现间存在鲁棒性差距（稳健性差距），且常出现正确最终输出但中间状态推理错误（表面执行）的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码推理基准仅评估单一规范实现下的最终输出正确性，忽略了模型在函数等价实现间的一致性（实现不变性）以及对中间执行状态的推理能力（过程透明性）。

Method: 构建CoRE（Code Reasoning）基准，从实现不变性（functionally equivalent implementations下的一致性）和过程透明性（中间执行状态推理）两个维度评估LLM的代码推理能力。

Result: 在8个前沿LLM上的评估发现两个根本性局限：(1) 稳健性差距：模型在不同等价实现间性能差异显著；(2) 表面执行：模型能输出正确最终结果，但中间状态推理错误。

Conclusion: 仅评估最终输出的方法不足以衡量代码推理能力，CoRE可作为评估鲁棒和忠实代码推理的必要基准。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on code generation tasks, it remains unclear whether large language models (LLMs) genuinely reason about code execution. Existing code reasoning benchmarks primarily evaluate final output correctness under a single canonical implementation, leaving two critical aspects underexplored: (1) whether LLMs can maintain consistency to functionally equivalent implementations, and (2) whether LLMs can accurately reason about intermediate execution states. We introduce \textbf{CoRE}, a \textbf{Co}de \textbf{Re}asoning benchmark that evaluates code reasoning through \textbf{implementation invariance} and \textbf{process transparency}. Extensive evaluations on eight frontier LLMs reveal two fundamental limitations. First, models exhibit a substantial \textbf{robustness gap}, with performance varying significantly across equivalent implementations. Second, we observe \textbf{superficial execution}, where models arrive at correct final outputs without correctly reasoning about intermediate execution states. Together, these findings demonstrate that output-only evaluations are insufficient for assessing code reasoning and position CoRE as a necessary benchmark for evaluating robust and faithful code reasoning.\footnote{Data and code are available at https://github.com/ZJUSig/CoRE.}

</details>


### [9] [Using Large Language Models for Black-Box Testing of FMU-Based Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25650)
*Abdullah Mughees,Gaadha Sudheerbabu,Tanwir Ahmad,Dragos Truscan,Mikael Manngård,Kristian Klemets*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种人在回路的黑盒测试方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）为功能样机单元（FMU）自动生成测试场景，减少手动定义仿真测试场景的工作量，并提升结果可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态仿真模型的黑盒测试中，手动定义测试场景耗时费力，且结果可解释性不足，亟需自动化方法来提升测试效率。

Method: 1) 输入FMU的功能与接口规范；2) 利用LLM生成Given-When-Then格式的结构化场景目标，定义初始条件、条件变化及期望输出；3) 生成场景计划，指定输入模式与断言预言；4) 生成完整输入时间序列，运行FMU仿真并评估断言；5) 输出可读日志、统计图表及聚合通过率；6) 存储场景与结果供后续评估与重执行。

Result: 在润滑油冷却系统上验证了该方法，结果表明LLM辅助的场景生成能够有效促进动态仿真模型的自动化测试设计与验证。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的场景生成方法可行且实用，可减少人为测试设计负担，提升测试效率与结果可解释性，适合日常工程使用。

Abstract: We propose a human in the loop approach for black-box testing of Functional Mock-up Units (FMUs) using Large Language Models (LLMs). The goal is to reduce the manual effort in defining test scenarios for dynamic simulation models and to improve the interpretability of results. The approach takes the functional and interface specifications of an FMU as input, and prompts an LLM to generate structured scenario goals in Given-When-Then format that define the initial input conditions of the simulation, a possible change in those conditions, and the expected output behaviour of the system against those changes. The corresponding scenario plans specify input patterns and add assertion oracles that describe expected output patterns defined in scenario goals. The approach generates a complete input time series for the scenario plans, runs the FMU simulation, and evaluates assertions on the recorded outputs. It produces human-readable logs and plots that show statistics for each scenario with overlays, aggregate pass rates, and per-goal outcomes. The generated scenarios and results are stored for evaluation and later re-execution. We evaluate the approach on a Lube Oil Cooling system and discuss design choices that make the approach practical for everyday use. Results suggest that LLM-assisted scenario generation can facilitate automatic test design and verification of dynamic simulation models.

</details>


### [10] [Recommending Usability Improvements with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25420)
*Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber,Viet-Man Le,Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的自动化可用性评估方法，通过屏幕录屏和有限应用上下文输入，自动识别可用性问题、提供基于尼尔森启发式规则的解释和改进建议，并按严重程度排序。用户研究表明该方法能提供低成本的可用性改进建议，是传统评估方法的有效补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可用性评估方法需要大量专业知识和资源，对小型团队和组织挑战较大。自动化可用性评估可降低成本、提高可及性，而多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的出现为此提供了新的技术机遇。

Method: 使用有限的应用上下文和用户交互屏幕录屏作为MLLM的输入，模型自动基于尼尔森可用性启发式规则识别和描述可用性问题，提供解释和改进建议，并按严重程度对建议进行排序。

Result: 通过以软件工程师为参与者的用户研究，评估了最高排名建议的质量和实用性，结果表明该方法能够提供低成本的可用性改进建议。

Conclusion: 该方法在缺乏可用性专家的场景下，可作为传统评估方法的有效补充，为未来集成到开发工具、实现在软件工程工作流程中的自动化可用性评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Usability describes quality attributes of application user interfaces that determine how effectively users can interact with them. Traditional usability evaluation methods require considerable expertise and resources, which can be challenging, especially for small teams and organizations. Automating usability evaluation could make it more accessible and help to improve the user experience. The recent emergence of powerful multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has opened new opportunities for automating usability evaluation and recommendation of improvements. These models can process visual inputs such as images and videos alongside textual context, which enables the identification of usability issues and the generation of actionable suggestions to resolve these issues.
  In this paper, we present a novel automated approach that uses limited application context and screen recordings of user interactions as input to an MLLM. The model automatically identifies and describes usability issues based on Nielsens usability heuristics, and provides corresponding explanations and improvement recommendations. To reduce the developer effort of manual prioritization, the recommendations are ranked by severity. The quality and practical usefulness of the generated recommendations were evaluated based on a user study that involved software engineers as participants. The evaluation focused on the highest-ranked suggestions provided by the model. The results demonstrate the potential of our approach to provide low-effort usability improvement recommendations. This makes it a promising complement to traditional evaluation methods, especially in settings with limited access to usability experts. In this sense, the approach serves as a basis for future integration into development tools to enable automated usability evaluation within software engineering workflows.

</details>


### [11] [AI as Consumer and Participant: A Co-Design Agenda for MBSE Substrates and Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25526)
*Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 当前AI工具在MBSE模型上运行，但模型本身被当作提示词而非知识库使用，导致不同工具产生不一致结果且无法裁决。本文提出模型与方法论需协同设计，使模型成为机器可查询的知识基板，并提出了三项原则以指导这一变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBSE模型并非为AI消费设计，AI工具从训练数据而非模型中提取推理，同一模型上的不同工具产生冲突结果而无记录可裁决，模型实际上充当提示词而非知识库。单纯附加更好工具无法解决此问题。

Method: 通过具体工作流场景展示当前差距，提出三项原则来共同刻画模型和方法论必须实现的目标。

Result: 识别出模型作为提示词而非知识库使用的根本问题，提出三项原则，并呼吁社区在AI集成的架构决策固化之前开始系统性工作。

Conclusion: 模型与方法论需要为AI参与而协同设计，将模型视为机器可查询的知识基板而非仅用于人类导航的结构化人工制品，这一共同设计尚未系统性地发生，亟需社区行动。

Abstract: AI tools are being deployed over MBSE models today, and those models were not designed for this kind of consumption. The problem is not simply that tools hallucinate: well-prompted frontier models produce competent, useful output over a conformant SysML model, but the reasoning they produce is drawn from training rather than retrieved from the model itself, and different tools over the same model produce different results with nothing in the record to adjudicate between them. The model, in other words, is functioning as a prompt rather than as a knowledge base. Attaching better tools to the same model does not resolve this. The model and the methodology that governs its construction need to be designed together for AI participation, treating the model as a machine-queryable knowledge substrate rather than a structured artefact for human navigation, and that co-design has not yet happened in any systematic way. This paper works through a concrete workflow scenario to show what that gap looks like in practice, proposes three principles that jointly characterise what model and methodology must achieve together, and closes with a call to the community to begin this work before the architectural decisions about AI integration settle without the methodological foundation they require.

</details>


### [12] [An Empirical Analysis of Mobile Energy Consumption Across User Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25587)
*Wellington Oliveira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过自动化监控框架，对超过12,000个数据点进行系统分析，量化了用户可控参数（如屏幕亮度、刷新率、连接状态、界面主题、省电模式及应用内设置）对移动设备能耗的实际影响，揭示了用户体验与电池续航之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节能建议多为泛泛而谈或聚焦软件开发层面，缺乏针对终端用户行为的学术严谨验证，用户缺乏可靠的量化依据来优化设备能耗。

Method: 采用自动化监控框架，模拟真实使用模式，在WhatsApp、Instagram、TikTok、YouTube等主流应用上执行系列用户界面测试，系统评估屏幕亮度、刷新率、连接状态、界面主题、省电模式及视频分辨率、消息大小等用户可控因素的能耗影响。

Result: 分析了超过12,000个数据点，量化了常见设置的真实能耗影响，揭示了用户体验与设备续航之间的具体权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本研究弥合了技术能耗分析与用户实际应用之间的鸿沟，为终端用户提供了基于数据的节能优化依据。

Abstract: Mobile devices have become ubiquitous tools for communication, entertainment, and productivity, yet battery autonomy remains a constraint. While energy-saving tips exist, they are often generic, anecdotal, or focused on software development rather than end-user behavior, leaving users to rely on grey literature or tacit knowledge to optimize their device energy consumption, lacking the academic rigor to ensure their effectiveness. This research aims to bridge the gap between technical energy analysis and practical user application by quantifying the energy consumption of different user-controlled parameters. Employing an automated monitoring framework, a series of user interface tests that simulate realistic usage patterns across popular applications (i.e., WhatsApp, Instagram, TikTok, and YouTube) was conducted. The objective is to have a systematic evaluation of the energy impact of user-controllable factors, including device settings, such as screen brightness, refresh rate, connectivity status, interface themes, and battery-saving profiles, combined with more app-specific variables (e.g., video resolution and message size). By analyzing over 12,000 data points, this paper quantifies the real-world impact of common settings, revealing the trade-offs between user experience and device autonomy.

</details>


### [13] [PLMGH: What Matters in PLM-GNN Hybrids for Code Classification and Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25599)
*Mohamed Taoufik Kaouthar El Idrissi,Edward Zulkoski,Mohammad Hamdaqa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种代码专用PLM与三种GNN架构的组合在代码分类和漏洞检测任务上的表现，发现PLM-GNN混合模型优于纯GNN模型，且PLM的选择比GNN对性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码理解模型越来越多地依赖PLM和GNN这两种互补的语义和结构信息编码方式，但缺乏对它们混合使用的系统理解和设计指导。

Method: 将三种代码专用PLM与三种基础GNN架构进行系统配对，在Java250和Devign数据集上（含标识符混淆设置）进行控制实验，与纯PLM和纯GNN基线对比。

Result: 混合模型一致优于纯GNN基线，常能改善冻结PLM的排序质量；在Devign上，PLM特征来源比GNN骨干对性能和鲁棒性影响更大；更大的PLM不一定提供更好的特征提取效果。

Conclusion: PLM选择比GNN选择对模型性能影响更大，并据此给出了PLM-GNN设计的实用指导原则。

Abstract: Code understanding models increasingly rely on pretrained language models (PLMs) and graph neural networks (GNNs), which capture complementary semantic and structural information. We conduct a controlled empirical study of PLM-GNN hybrids for code classification and vulnerability detection tasks by systematically pairing three code-specialized PLMs with three foundational GNN architectures. We compare these hybrids against PLM-only and GNN-only baselines on Java250 and Devign, including an identifier-obfuscation setting. Across both tasks, hybrids consistently outperform GNN-only baselines and often improve ranking quality over frozen PLMs. On Devign, performance and robustness are more sensitive to the PLM feature source than to the GNN backbone. We also find that larger PLMs are not necessarily better feature extractors in this pipeline, and that the PLM choice has more impact than the GNN choice. Finally, we distill these findings into practical guidelines for PLM-GNN design choices in code classification and vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [14] [Spreadsheet Modeling Experiments Using GPTs on Small Problem Statements and the Wall Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25689)
*Thomas A. Grossman,Yuan Chen,Sopiko Datuashvili*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPT工具在构建可复用的分析性电子表格模型中的应用，通过对五个GPT扩展的筛选和Excel AI的详细测试，发现虽然能生成结构良好的模型，但存在不一致性和不可复现性问题，当前工具尚不可靠用于专业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GPT工具辅助构建可复用分析性电子表格模型的可行性，评估其实际表现与局限性。

Method: 筛选五个GPT扩展，选取Excel AI (pulsrai.com)进行详细测试；设计结构化实验，基于简单问题陈述，依据ERFR标准（单格单输入、单元格公式、无硬编码数字、标签、准确性）评估性能。

Result: Excel AI能生成结构良好的模型，但结果不一致且常不可复现；识别出两大核心挑战："置信度问题"和"工作流问题"。

Conclusion: GPT工具在生成草稿模型方面有潜力，可能减少开发时间或降低技能要求，但当前工具对专业使用仍不可靠；建议未来研究关注提示工程、可复现性及更大规模建模任务。

Abstract: This paper investigates how GPT-based tools can assist in building reusable analytical spreadsheet models. After a screening, we evaluate five GPT extensions and select Excel AI by pulsrai.com for detailed testing. Through structured experiments on simple problem statements, we assess Excel AI's performance against the ERFR criteria (each input in a cell; cell formulas; no hardwired numbers; labels; accurate). Results show that while Excel AI can produce well-structured models, it is inconsistent and often non-reproducible. We identify two central challenges - "the problem of confidence" and "the problem of workflow" - which highlight the need for skilled users to verify and adapt GPT-generated spreadsheets. Though GPTs show promise for generating draft models that may reduce development time or lower skill requirements, current tools remain unreliable for professional use. We conclude with recommendations for future research into prompt engineering, reproducibility, and larger-scale modeling tasks.

</details>


### [15] [Bug-Report-Driven Fault Localization: Industrial Benchmarking and Lesson Learned at ABB Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25700)
*Pernilla Hall,Anton Ununger,Riccardo Rubei,Alessio Bucaioni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用基于自然语言文本的监督分类方法，利用历史缺陷报告进行故障定位，在ABB Robotics的工业数据集上，传统机器学习模型（尤其是TF-IDF特征）优于微调后的Transformer模型，数据增强可提升随机森林性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中大规模长期系统不可避免地积累缺陷，而传统故障定位依赖代码级或运行时上下文，成本高且耗时长；开发者主要依靠文本缺陷报告进行维护，因此需要一种仅基于自然语言内容的、可直接部署于现有工业工作流的故障定位方法。

Method: 将故障定位建模为监督文本分类问题，评估三种传统模型（逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林）和两种微调Transformer模型（RoBERTa-Base、Distil-RoBERTa），使用TF-IDF特征及数据增强，在ABB Robotics五年工业缺陷报告数据集上进行实验。

Result: 传统模型结合TF-IDF特征在所有评估指标上一致优于微调的Transformer模型；数据增强提升了随机森林的性能。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在工业领域特定数据上并非普遍优于经典方法；历史缺陷报告可系统用于基于文本的AI辅助故障定位，提供可扩展、低成本且基于实证的工业调试补充方案。

Abstract: Software quality assurance remains a major challenge in industrial environments, where large-scale and long-lived systems inevitably accumulate defects. Identifying the location of a fault is often time-consuming and costly, particularly during maintenance phases when developers must rely primarily on textual bug reports rather than complete runtime or code-level context. In this study, we investigated if artificial intelligence can support fault localization using only the natural-language content of bug reports. By relying only on textual information, our approach requires no access to source code, execution traces, or static analysis artifacts, making it directly deployable within existing industrial maintenance workflows. We framed fault localization as a supervised text classification problem and evaluated three traditional machine learning models (Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine, and Random Forest) and two fine-tuned transformer-based language models (RoBERTa-Base and Distil-RoBERTa). Our evaluation used proprietary data from ABB Robotics in Sweden, comprising five years of resolved industrial bug reports, each linked to its verified code fix. This setting allowed us to assess model effectiveness under realistic industrial constraints.
  Our results showed that traditional models using term frequency-inverse document features consistently outperformed the fine-tuned language models on this dataset, while data augmentation improved Random Forest performance. These findings challenge the assumption that transformer-based models universally outperform classical approaches in industrial contexts with domain-specific data. We demonstrated that historical bug reports can be systematically used for text-based, artificial intelligence-assisted fault localization, providing a scalable, low-cost, and empirically grounded complement to common debugging practices in industry.

</details>


### [16] [Learning Generalizable Multimodal Representations for Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25711)
*Zeming Dong,Yuejun Guo,Qiang Hu,Yao Zhang,Maxime Cordy,Hao Liu,Mike Papadakis,Yongqiang Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出多模态对比框架MultiVul，通过双相似度学习和一致性正则化对齐代码与注释表示，在多个LLM上实现最多27.07%的F1提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞检测方法多依赖单模态代码表示，忽略了注释中蕴含的互补语义信息（开发者意图），限制了模型对复杂代码结构和逻辑关系的泛化能力。

Method: 提出MultiVul多模态对比框架，通过双相似度学习（dual similarity learning）和一致性正则化（consistency regularization）对齐代码与注释表示，并利用多样化代码-文本对增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在DiverseVul和Devign数据集上，基于DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、StarCoder2-7B和CodeLlama-7B四个LLM，相比提示方法F1提升最高27.07%，相比仅代码微调提升13.37%，推理效率相当。

Conclusion: 利用代码与注释的多模态对齐能显著提升漏洞检测性能，且不牺牲推理效率。

Abstract: Source code and its accompanying comments are complementary yet naturally aligned modalities-code encodes structural logic while comments capture developer intent. However, existing vulnerability detection methods mostly rely on single-modality code representations, overlooking the complementary semantic information embedded in comments and thus limiting their generalization across complex code structures and logical relationships. To address this, we propose MultiVul, a multimodal contrastive framework that aligns code and comment representations through dual similarity learning and consistency regularization, augmented with diverse code-text pairs to improve robustness. Experiments on widely adopted DiverseVul and Devign datasets across four large language models (LLMs) (i.e., DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B, StarCoder2-7B, and CodeLlama-7B) show that MultiVul achieves up to 27.07% F1 improvement over prompting-based methods and 13.37% over code-only Fine-Tuning, while maintaining comparable inference efficiency.

</details>


### [17] [SAFEdit: Does Multi-Agent Decomposition Resolve the Reliability Challenges of Instructed Code Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25737)
*Noam Tarshish,Nofar Selouk,Daniel Hodisan,Bar Ezra Gafniel,Yuval Elovici,Asaf Shabtai,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SAFEdit是一种多智能体框架，通过分解为规划器、编辑器和验证器三个专门角色，并引入失败抽象层（FAL），在EditBench基准上实现了68.6%的任务成功率，比单模型基线高3.8个百分点，比ReAct单智能体基线高8.6个百分点，迭代精炼贡献了17.4个百分点的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在指令驱动的代码编辑任务上表现不佳，EditBench基准中39/40的模型任务成功率低于60%，表明通用代码生成与指令驱动编辑之间存在显著差距，急需一种能够提高可靠性的框架。

Method: 提出SAFEdit多智能体框架，包含三个角色：(1) Planner Agent生成显式、可见性感知的编辑计划；(2) Editor Agent执行最小、文字级别的代码修改；(3) Verifier Agent运行真实测试。当测试失败时，通过Failure Abstraction Layer (FAL)将原始测试日志转换为结构化诊断反馈，反馈给Editor进行迭代精炼。

Result: 在EditBench上对445个代码编辑实例（5种语言、多种空间上下文变体）评估，SAFEdit达到68.6% TSR，优于单模型基线3.8个百分点，优于ReAct单智能体基线8.6个百分点。迭代精炼贡献了17.4个百分点的成功率，指令级幻觉相比单智能体方法有所减少。

Conclusion: SAFEdit通过角色分解和结构化失败反馈证明了多智能体框架在指令驱动代码编辑任务中的有效性，不仅提升了任务成功率，还通过自动错误分析提供了超越二元通过/失败结果的失败解释能力。

Abstract: Instructed code editing is a significant challenge for large language models (LLMs). On the EditBench benchmark, 39 of 40 evaluated models obtain a task success rate (TSR) below 60 percent, highlighting a gap between general code generation and the ability to perform instruction-driven editing under executable test constraints. To address this, we propose SAFEdit, a multi-agent framework for instructed code editing that decomposes the editing process into specialized roles to improve reliability and reduce unintended code changes. A Planner Agent produces an explicit, visibility-aware edit plan, an Editor Agent applies minimal, literal code modifications, and a Verifier Agent executes real test runs. When tests fail, SAFEdit uses a Failure Abstraction Layer (FAL) to transform raw test logs into structured diagnostic feedback, which is fed back to the Editor to support iterative refinement. We compare SAFEdit against both prior single-model results reported for EditBench and an implemented ReAct single-agent baseline under the same evaluation conditions. We used EditBench to evaluate SAFEdit on 445 code editing instances in five languages (English, Polish, Spanish, Chinese, and Russian) under varying spatial context variants. SAFEdit achieved 68.6 percent TSR, outperforming the single-model baseline by 3.8 percentage points and the ReAct single-agent baseline by 8.6 percentage points. The iterative refinement loop was found to contribute 17.4 percentage points to SAFEdit's overall success rate. SAFEdit's automated error analysis further indicates a reduction in instruction-level hallucinations compared to single-agent approaches, providing an additional framework component for interpreting failures beyond pass or fail outcomes.

</details>


### [18] [Scenario-based System Testing for Distributed Robotics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25772)
*Jan Peleska,Felix Brüning,Wen-Ling Huang,Anne E. Haxthausen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向复杂分布式系统的场景规约语言(SCSL)，支持系统级测试的自动生成与执行。SCSL通过场景组合、在线测试和动态协作重构，解决了传统模型测试在分布式系统中因复杂度高、非确定性大和动态配置变化而面临的三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的测试方法在复杂分布式系统（如协作自主机器人）中变得不切实际，原因包括：(1) 整体系统复杂度过高，难以建立单一整体模型；(2) 分布式系统存在大量非确定性，测试行为无法完全预计算；(3) 系统配置可能在运行时动态变化。

Method: 提出SCSL语言，核心包含：(1) 场景（Scenario）机制，每个场景描述测试特定的预期行为或激励，支持顺序和并行组合以构建复杂测试；(2) 在线(on-the-fly)测试平台，在运行时选择和执行测试步骤以应对非确定性；(3) 协作（Collaboration）构件，支持动态重配置，包括移除不可用组件、注册新加入组件、在测试执行期间重连接口。

Result: 通过机器人执行打捞任务的系统测试示例展示了SCSL的语法和语义，并使用原型工具平台自动生成的测试执行来验证所支持的概念。

Conclusion: SCSL语言及其工具平台能够有效应对复杂分布式系统测试中的三大挑战：通过场景组合降低模型复杂度，通过在线测试处理非确定性，通过协作构件实现运行时动态重配置。

Abstract: We present the SCenario Specification Language (SCSL) for automated generation and execution of system-level tests. SCSL targets complex distributed systems (e.g., collaborating autonomous robots) where classical model-based testing becomes impractical because (1) the overall system complexity is too high for a single monolithic model, (2) test behaviour cannot be fully precomputed due to substantial nondeterminism in the distributed system under test (SUT), and (3) the SUT configuration may change dynamically at runtime. Challenge (1) is addressed by scenarios: each scenario specifies test-specific expected SUT behaviour and/or stimuli to be applied during execution. Complex system tests are composed from elementary scenarios using sequential and parallel composition. To address (2), the SCSL tool platform supports online (on-the-fly) testing, selecting and executing test steps during runtime. For (3), SCSL provides a collaboration construct that supports dynamic reconfiguration: removing unavailable components, registering newly joining components, and rewiring interfaces during test execution. We illustrate the syntax and semantics of SCSL using a system-test example in which robots perform a salvage mission, and we use an automatically generated test execution to demonstrate the concepts supported by our prototype tool platform.

</details>


### [19] [Can Code Evaluation Metrics Detect Code Plagiarism?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25778)
*Fahad Ebrahim,Mike Joy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对比实验探究代码评估指标（CEMs）在源代码抄袭检测中的有效性，发现CrystalBLEU在特定预处理和难度级别上可媲美甚至超越专用检测工具Dolos和JPlag。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码评估指标（CEMs）已被广泛用于代码生成任务评估，但它们能否有效检测不同修改级别（L1-L6）的抄袭行为尚不明确，因此需要系统性实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 使用ConPlag（含raw和template-free版本）和IRPlag两个开源标注数据集，评估CodeBLEU、CrystalBLEU、RUBY、TSED和CodeBERTScore五种CEMs，采用无阈值排序指标与SOTA工具JPlag和Dolos进行对比。

Result: 无预处理时Dolos整体最优，但CrystalBLEU、CodeBLEU和RUBY优于JPlag；有预处理时CrystalBLEU超越Dolos。性能在L1最强，L4起下降，CrystalBLEU在L6仍有竞争力。CrystalBLEU在多个数据集和难度级别上表现领先。

Conclusion: 代码评估指标（CEMs）在排序指标上与专用抄袭检测工具具有可比性，表明CEMs可有效应用于源代码抄袭检测领域。

Abstract: Source Code Plagiarism Detection (SCPD) plays an important role in maintaining fairness and academic integrity in software engineering education. Code Evaluation Metrics (CEMs) are developed for assessing code generation tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such metrics can reliably detect plagiarism across different levels of modification (L1-L6), increasing in complexity.
  In this paper, we perform a comparative empirical study using two open-source labelled datasets, ConPlag (raw and template-free versions) and IRPlag. We evaluate five CEMs, namely CodeBLEU, CrystalBLEU, RUBY, Tree Structured Edit Distance (TSED), and CodeBERTScore. The performance is evaluated using threshold-free ranking-based measures to assess overall, per dataset, and per-level plagiarism performance. The results are compared against state-of-the-art (SOTA) Source Code Plagiarism Detection Tools (SCPDTs), JPlag and Dolos.
  Our findings show that without preprocessing, Dolos achieves the highest overall ranking performance, while among the individual metrics, CrystalBLEU, CodeBLEU, and RUBY outperform JPlag. Performance is strongest at L1 and drops from L4 onward, while CrystalBLEU remains competitive on L6. With preprocessing, CrystalBLEU surpasses Dolos overall. Per dataset, Dolos achieved the best ranking on the ConPlag raw dataset, while CrystalBLEU was the best-performing metric on the remaining datasets. At the plagiarism levels, Dolos remains strongest on L4, while Crystal-BLEU leads most of the remaining difficult levels. These results indicate that CEMs are comparable to dedicated tools in terms of ranking metrics.

</details>


### [20] [Key Developer Roles and Organizational Coupling in Microservices: A Longitudinal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25804)
*Xiaozhou Li,Nariman Mani,Jose Sosa Rodriguez,Tomas Cerny*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对大规模微服务系统的纵向仓库挖掘，发现组织耦合（OC）主要受开发者角色驱动，而非架构的固有属性。Connector角色与高OC持续相关，多角色共存会放大耦合效应，而Jack和Maven角色影响更局部化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统带来了显著的组织协调挑战，但个体开发者在塑造组织耦合（OC）中的作用尚未被充分探索。现有研究主要关注结构化的架构方面，对开发者角色如何随时间影响协调动态缺乏理解。

Method: 对大规模微服务系统进行纵向仓库挖掘分析，利用GitHub数据（包括提交记录、问题和拉取请求交互）来操作化组织耦合（OC）并量化其随时间演化。聚焦三种关键角色：Jacks（广泛知识持有者）、Mavens（深度专家）和Connectors（组织桥梁）。

Result: Connectors始终与较高水平的OC相关；同一开发者内多角色共存进一步放大耦合效应；Jacks和Mavens表现出更局部化和角色特定的影响。

Conclusion: 微服务中的OC主要是一种角色驱动现象，而非不可避免的结构属性，这为角色感知的组织设计和有针对性的解耦策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Microservice-based systems impose significant organizational coordination challenges, yet the role of individual developers in shaping organizational coupling (OC) remains underexplored. Prior work largely focuses on structural architectural aspects, leaving gaps in understanding how developer roles influence coordination dynamics over time. This study investigates how different developer roles contribute to OC in a large-scale microservices system. The analysis focuses on three key roles, namely Jacks, representing broad knowledge holders, Mavens, representing deep specialists, and Connectors, representing organizational bridges. A longitudinal repository mining analysis of GitHub data, including commits and issue and pull request interactions, is conducted to operationalize OC and quantify its evolution over time. The results show that Connectors are consistently associated with higher levels of OC, while the co-occurrence of multiple roles within the same developer further amplifies coupling effects. In contrast, Jacks and Mavens exhibit more localized and role-specific influences. These findings indicate that OC in microservices is primarily a role-driven phenomenon rather than an inevitable structural property, providing a foundation for role-aware organizational design and targeted decoupling strategies.

</details>


### [21] [Does social identity matter in software engineering? Assessing the case of research software engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25831)
*Chukwudi Uwasomba,Tamara Lopez,Melanie Langer,Helen Sharp,Michel Wermelinger,Caroline Jay,Mark Levine,Bashar Nuseibeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用混合方法（计算语言学分析、推论统计），分析了超过28,000条社交媒体帖子、1,700篇博客和381份调查问卷，探索研究软件工程师（RSE）群体中的社会身份认同，发现集体RSE身份的出现及其对职业幸福感的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会身份是心理学中源于群体归属感的个人身份组成部分，本研究旨在探索研究软件工程师（RSE）这一专业社区成员中的社会身份认同。

Method: 采用混合研究方法，结合计算语言学分析和推论统计学，对超过28,000条社交媒体帖子、1,700篇博客以及381名专业RSE的问卷调查回复进行分析。

Result: 发现了集体RSE身份认同的出现，并证明了这种身份认同在塑造职业幸福感方面的作用。

Conclusion: 本研究通过整合社会心理学和软件工程，展示了专业身份如何演变及其重要性，提供了跨学科的视角。

Abstract: Social identity is a concept from psychology that refers to the part of an individual's identity that derives from their group membership(s). In this paper, we explore social identity in members of the professional community of Research Software Engineers (RSEs). Using a mixed-methods approach, our study combined computational linguistic analysis and inferential statistics to examine over 28,000 social media posts, 1,700 blogs, and survey responses from 381 professional RSEs. The findings highlight the emergence of a collective RSE identity and demonstrate its role in shaping professional wellbeing. This study contributes an interdisciplinary perspective by integrating social psychology and software engineering to show how a professional identity evolves and why it matters.

</details>


### [22] [RESTestBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLM-Generated REST API Test Cases from NL Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25862)
*Leon Kogler,Stefan Hangler,Maximilian Ehrhart,Benedikt Dornauer,Roland Wuersching,Peter Schrammel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RESTestBench基准，包含三个REST服务及人工验证的自然语言需求（精确和模糊变体），引入基于需求的变异测试度量来评估测试生成的有效性。实验表明，与故障或变异代码交互会显著降低测试有效性，尤其是模糊需求，且高细节需求下无需实际SUT行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REST API测试工具通常用代码覆盖率和崩溃率评估，但基于LLM的测试生成方法从自然语言需求生成测试以验证功能行为，传统指标无法衡量生成的测试是否验证了预期行为。

Method: 构建RESTestBench基准，包含三个REST服务和人工验证的自然语言需求（精确和模糊变体）。引入基于需求的变异测试度量，基于Bartocci等人的属性方法扩展。评估两种方法：非细化生成和通过与运行中SUT交互指导的细化生成。

Result: 与故障/变异代码交互时测试有效性显著下降（尤其模糊需求），有时抵消细化收益；需求细节高时无需实际SUT行为。

Conclusion: RESTestBench能有效评估基于需求的测试生成，揭示了LLM生成测试时与SUT交互的潜在负面影响。

Abstract: Existing REST API testing tools are typically evaluated using code coverage and crash-based fault metrics. However, recent LLM-based approaches increasingly generate tests from NL requirements to validate functional behaviour, making traditional metrics weak proxies for whether generated tests validate intended behaviour. To address this gap, we present RESTestBench, a benchmark comprising three REST services paired with manually verified NL requirements in both precise and vague variants, enabling controlled and reproducible evaluation of requirement-based test generation. RESTestBench further introduces a requirements-based mutation testing metric that measures the fault-detection effectiveness of a generated test case with respect to a specific requirement, extending the property-based approach of Bartocci et al. . Using RESTestBench, we evaluate two approaches across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: (i) non-refinement-based generation, and (ii) refinement-based generation guided by interaction with the running SUT. In the refinement experiments, RESTestBench assesses how exposure to the actual implementation, valid or mutated, affects test effectiveness. Our results show that test effectiveness drops considerably when the generator interacts with faulty or mutated code, especially for vague requirements, sometimes negating the benefit of refinement and indicating that incorporating actual SUT behaviour is unnecessary when requirement detail is high.

</details>


### [23] [From Threads to Trajectories: A Multi-LLM Pipeline for Community Knowledge Extraction from GitHub Issue Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25880)
*Nazia Shehnaz Joynab,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-MIMIC-Bench，一个从GitHub讨论中利用多LLM流水线自动生成结构化问题轨迹（trajectory）的数据集，通过细粒度分析评论、分类标签并合成连贯叙事，在800个真实问题上达到91.7%的提取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型开源项目中的复杂问题处理需要开发者阅读大量非结构化、碎片化的讨论线程，认知负担极重，亟需一种自动化方法将混乱的讨论转化为结构化的推理轨迹以降低理解成本。

Method: 构建自动化多LLM流水线，使用五种闭源LLM配置分别执行标签分类、内联代码块和外部链接摘要、评论分析、标签特定字段分类及轨迹合成，实现从原始评论到标签感知轨迹的端到端生成。

Result: 在来自SWE-Bench-Pro、SWE-Bench-Multilingual和SWE-Bench-Verified的800个真实GitHub问题上评估，成功提取734个高保真推理轨迹，成功率达91.7%。

Conclusion: 该流水线能生成简洁可靠的轨迹，辅助开发者理解经验驱动的协作式问题诊断过程，并可用于训练LLM智能体模仿专家开发者的推理行为。

Abstract: Resolution of complex post-production issues in large-scale open-source software (OSS) projects requires significant cognitive effort, as developers need to go through long, unstructured and fragmented issue discussion threads before that. In this paper, we present SWE-MIMIC-Bench, an issue trajectory dataset generated from raw GitHub discussions using an automated multi-LLM pipeline. Unlike simple summarization, this pipeline utilizes a group of closed-source LLMs to perform granular tasks: analyzing individual comments with awareness of externally-linked resources, classifying comment analyses into label-specific fields (e.g., root cause, solution plan, implementation progress), and synthesizing label-aware trajectories which capture a structured and coherent narrative of the entire discussion thread. Our pipeline uses five closed-source LLM configurations for distinct purposes: label classification, inline code block and external link summarization, comment analysis, label-specific field classification and trajectory synthesis. By generating concise and reliable trajectories from complex conversation threads, this system can assist developers and researchers of broader software engineering community to understand the experience-driven collaborative approach for issue diagnosis. Furthermore, the generated trajectories can be used to train modern LLM agents to think and act like an expert developer. We evaluated our system on 800 real-world GitHub issues drawn from the SWE-Bench-Pro, SWE-Bench-Multilingual and SWE-Bench-Verified dataset, achieving a 91.7% success rate in extracting 734 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [24] [Carbon-Taxed Transformers: A Green Compression Pipeline for Overgrown Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25903)
*Ajmain Inqiad Alam,Palash Roy,Chanchal K. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出碳税变压器(CTT)，一种受经济碳定价启发的多架构压缩流水线方法，在代码克隆检测、摘要和生成三个SE任务上，实现了高达49倍内存缩减、8-10倍速度提升、81%碳排放降低，同时保持约89-98%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程中应用日益广泛，但其巨大的模型规模导致部署缓慢、内存密集、碳排放高，严重威胁AI驱动SE的可扩展性、可访问性和环境可持续性。现有研究过度关注准确率，忽视了效率和环境成本。

Method: 借鉴经济碳定价概念，设计CTT多架构压缩流水线：将计算碳税作为约束，惩罚低效架构、奖励可部署压缩。通过系统化的流水线排序与组合，对编码器-only、编码器-解码器、解码器-only三类架构进行压缩。

Result: 在三个SE任务上：(1) 内存缩减最高49倍；(2) 推理时间：克隆检测8-10倍、摘要3倍、代码生成4-7倍缩减；(3) CO2排放降低81%；(4) 准确率保持：克隆检测约98%、摘要约89%、生成文本指标91%和pass@1达68%。消融实验验证了流水线排序和各组分的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过激进但保持性能的压缩策略，CTT为SE领域实现负责任、可持续的AI提供了可行路径，证明效率和环境成本应作为AI系统的首要设计约束。

Abstract: The accelerating adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software engineering (SE) has brought with it a silent crisis: unsustainable computational cost. While these models demonstrate remarkable capabilities in different SE tasks, they are unmanageably large, slow to deploy, memory-intensive, and carbon-heavy. This reality threatens not only the scalability and accessibility of AI-powered SE, but also its long-term environmental sustainability. The research challenge is clear: we must go beyond accuracy and address efficiency and environmental cost as first-class design constraints. To meet this challenge, we introduce Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT), a systematic multi-architectural compression principled pipeline ordering inspired by economic carbon taxation principles. Drawing from the economic concept of carbon pricing, CTT operationalizes a computational carbon tax that penalizes architectural inefficiencies and rewards deployment-ready compression. We evaluate CTT across three core SE tasks: code clone detection, code summarization, and code generation, with models spanning encoder-only, encoder-decoder, and decoder-only architecture. Our results show that CTT delivers on inference: (1) up to 49x memory reduction, (2) time reduction up to 8-10x for clone detection, up to 3x for summarization, and 4-7x for generation, (3) up to 81% reduction in CO2 emissions and (4) CTT retains around 98% accuracy on clone detection, around 89% on summarization, and up to 91% (textual metrics) and 68% (pass@1) for generation. Two ablation studies show that pipeline ordering and individual component contributions are both essential, providing empirical justification for CTT's design and effectiveness. This work establishes a viable path toward responsible AI in SE through aggressive yet performance-preserving compression.

</details>

### [25] [Chorusing Synchronization Signals for Ambient 5G Backscatter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25641)
*Yunyun Feng,Chenhong Cao,Si Chen,Wei Gong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对称差分（SD）的同步方法，利用5G主同步信号（PSS）包络的镜像对称特性，实现高精度、低功耗、资源高效的同步，相比现有方法大幅降低D触发器资源消耗（低至30-181倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G反向散射通信中，传统同步方法（自相关/互相关）资源开销大，且基于互相关的资源优化方法因5G多模板问题而失效，亟需一种兼具高精度与低功耗的同步策略。

Method: 利用5G PSS包络的独特镜像对称特性，设计对称差分（SD）技术实现低功耗PSS检测，通过差分处理降低资源消耗。

Result: SD仅消耗3,175个D触发器，比NR细定时（NFT）低87倍，比基于对称的半模板同步（SST）低181倍，比对称自相关（SA）同步低30倍。在反向散射硬件、SDR gNodeB和UE组成的测试平台上进行了评估。

Conclusion: SD方法在5G反向散射通信中实现了高精度与资源高效的双重优势，为无线反向散射通信提供了有吸引力的同步解决方案。

Abstract: 5G backscatter communication presents an emerging energy-efficient IoT connectivity solution with enhanced availability and data rate advantages over traditional wireless networks. For 5G backscatter, synchronization is crucial as it ensures high-quality transmission. Popular synchronization methods employ autocorrelation and cross-correlation for accurate timing, yet they are constrained by resources. Traditional cross-correlation-based methods for resource utilization optimization also fail in 5G backscatter due to the presence of multiple templates for 5G. A synchronization strategy that supports high accuracy and low power would be highly attractive for wireless backscatter communication. We propose Symmetric Differential (SD)-based Sync, an accurate and resource-efficient synchronization method for 5G backscatter. We have observed that the envelope of the 5G Primary Synchronization Signal (PSS) exhibits a unique mirror symmetry, which enables us to employ differential techniques for low-power PSS detection. We extensively evaluated our design using a testbed of backscatter hardware, SDR gNodeB, and User Equipment (UE). Results show that our SD consumes 3,175 D flip-flops, which is 87x lower than NR fine timing (NFT), 181x lower than symmetry-based semi-template sync (SST), and 30x lower than symmetric autocorrelation (SA)-based sync.

</details>


### [26] [EOS-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25782)
*Qian Yin,Jiaxing Li,Jiaqi Cheng,Qizhang Luo,Annalisa Riccardi,Abhijit Chatterjee,Rafael Vazquez,Carlo Novara,Michalis Mavrovouniotis,Ponnuthurai Nagaratnam Suganthan,Shengzhou Bai,Xiaoxuan Hu,Lining Xing,Ming Xu,Shuang Li,Zixuan Zheng,Xin Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Yi Gu,Yanjie Song,Witold Pedrycz,Evan L. Kramer,Laio Oriel Seman,Cletah Shoko,Guohua Wu,Xinwei Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EOS-Bench，一个用于地球观测卫星成像调度的统一开源基准框架，包含1,390个场景和13,900个实例，支持从小规模到大规模（1000颗卫星、10000个请求）的调度评估，并引入场景特征化方案和多维评估协议，验证了混合整数规划、启发式、元启发式及深度强化学习等多种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星成像调度是一个NP难组合优化问题，但缺乏统一、开源的基准来比较不同算法，现有研究难以系统性和可重复性地评估调度方法。

Method: 1) 构建EOS-Bench框架，集成高保真轨道动力学与平台约束，生成1,390个场景和13,900个基准实例（规模从验证级到1000颗卫星、10000个请求的协作问题）；2) 提出场景特征化方案，从机会密度、任务灵活性、冲突强度、卫星拥塞度量化结构难度；3) 引入多维评估协议，涵盖任务收益、完成率、工作负载均衡、时效性和运行时间五个指标；4) 使用混合整数规划、启发式、元启发式和深度强化学习在敏捷与非敏捷设置下进行评测。

Result: EOS-Bench能够有效区分不同求解器在不同规模和条件下的性能，揭示了求解质量与计算效率之间的权衡，并提供了对场景复杂性的深入洞察。

Conclusion: EOS-Bench为地球观测卫星调度研究提供了一个统一、可扩展的开源测试平台，代码和数据已公开在GitHub上，有助于推动该领域的可重复性研究和算法比较。

Abstract: Earth observation satellite imaging scheduling is a challenging NP-hard combinatorial optimisation problem central to space mission operations. While next-generation agile Earth observation satellites (EOS) increase operational flexibility, they also significantly raise scheduling complexity. The lack of a unified, open-source benchmark makes it difficult to compare algorithms across studies. This paper introduces EOS-Bench, a comprehensive framework for systematic and reproducible evaluation of scheduling methods. By integrating high-fidelity orbital dynamics and platform constraints, EOS-Bench generates 1,390 scenarios and 13,900 benchmark instances, spanning from small-scale validation cases to large coordination problems with up to 1,000 satellites and 10,000 requests.
  We further propose a scenario characterisation scheme to quantify structural difficulty based on factors such as opportunity density, task flexibility, conflict intensity, and satellite congestion. A multidimensional evaluation protocol is introduced, assessing performance across five metrics: task profit, completion rate, workload balance, timeliness, and runtime. The framework is evaluated using mixed-integer programming, heuristics, meta-heuristics, and deep reinforcement learning across both agile and non-agile settings. Results show that EOS-Bench effectively distinguishes solver performance across scales and conditions, revealing trade-offs between solution quality and computational efficiency, and providing deeper insight into scenario complexity.
  EOS-Bench offers a unified and extensible open testbed for advancing research in Earth observation satellite scheduling. The code and data are available at https://github.com/Ethan19YQ/EOS-Bench.

</details>
