### [27] [Poisoning Learned Index Structures: Static and Dynamic Adversarial Attacks on ALEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24975)
*Allen Jue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ALEX学习索引在两种攻击范式（静态数据投毒和动态算法复杂度攻击）下的鲁棒性，发现动态ACA可造成2-2.8倍查询吞吐量下降，但效果高度依赖数据分布；静态投毒在大规模批量加载场景下影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习索引依赖数据分布的CDF建模，这引入了对对抗性操纵的潜在脆弱性。现有工作分别研究了静态投毒和动态ACA，但评估规模有限且往往只考虑单一威胁模型，缺乏统一的系统性研究。

Method: 在统一可复现框架下，对ALEX动态学习索引同时进行静态投毒和动态ACA攻击实验，使用多达20万次对抗性插入，涵盖多个SOSD数据集及真实键基线，进行可扩展的真实负载评估。

Result: 静态投毒在大规模批量加载下对ALEX查询性能影响极小；动态ACA可导致2-2.8倍查询吞吐量下降，但攻击有效性高度依赖数据集——密集键分布因重复插入多和ALEX局部化结构而限制攻击效果。

Conclusion: 学习索引的鲁棒性关键取决于威胁模型、数据分布和评估方法之间的相互作用；需关注控制负载的必要性以及局部结构损伤与全局查询指标之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Learned index structures achieve high performance by modeling the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of keys, but this reliance on data distributions introduces potential vulnerability to adversarial manipulation. Prior work has explored both static data poisoning and dynamic algorithmic complexity attacks (ACA), though evaluations are typically limited in scale or consider only one threat model. We present a systematic study of both attack paradigms on ALEX, a state-of-the-art dynamic learned index, under a unified and reproducible framework.
  Our evaluation scales to realistic workloads with up to 200K adversarial inserts and includes multiple SOSD datasets with diverse key distributions, as well as a real-key baseline to isolate adversarial effects. Our results show a clear separation between threat models. Static poisoning has minimal impact on lookup performance in ALEX under bulk-loaded settings, while dynamic ACA induces substantial degradation, with up to 2--2.8x slowdown in lookup throughput. However, attack effectiveness is highly dataset-dependent: dense key distributions limit adversarial leverage due to duplicate-heavy insertions and ALEX's localized structure.
  We highlight key evaluation considerations, including the need for control workloads and the mismatch between localized structural damage and global query metrics. These results show that robustness in learned indexes depends critically on the interaction between threat model, data distribution, and evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [28] [Semantic Denial of Service in LLM-controlled robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24790)
*Jonathan Steinberg,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了面向安全的人形机器人指令跟随系统存在一种可用性攻击面：攻击者通过向机器人音频通道注入简短的安全相关短语（1-5个token），即可触发模型的安全推理，导致执行中断或停止，而无需越狱模型或覆盖其策略。这种语义拒绝服务攻击在多种视觉-语言模型、提示级防御、部署模式及注入设置下均有效。提示级防御虽可降低部分模型的硬停止攻击成功率，但会将硬停止转化为确认循环和错误警报（以DSR衡量）。实际启示是：将未经验证的音频文本直接输入LLM的架构设计创建了安全监控与动作选择之间可避免的安全依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注如何让LLM控制机器人安全地遵循指令，但忽略了安全导向的指令跟随机制本身可能被利用作为攻击面。作者旨在揭示这种看似有益的安全机制实际上会引入一种新的可用性攻击向量。

Method: 通过向机器人音频通道注入简短的安全相关短语（1-5 tokens），测试四种视觉-语言模型、七种提示级防御、三种部署模式以及单次和多次注入设置下的攻击效果。使用硬停止攻击成功率（Hard-Stop Attack Success Rate）和干扰成功率（Disruption Success Rate, DSR）作为评估指标。

Result: 1) 短的安全相关短语注入即可触发模型安全推理，导致执行中断；2) 提示级防御虽能降低部分模型的硬停止攻击成功率，但会将硬停止转化为确认循环和错误警报；3) 注入多样性比重复相同短语更有效，表明模型将多样化的安全线索视为佐证证据；4) 防御手段改变了干扰的形式，而非消除干扰本身。

Conclusion: 问题的根本在于架构层面：将未经验证的音频文本直接输入LLM的架构设计，在安全监控与动作选择之间创建了不必要的安全依赖。因此，解决方案应着重于架构层面的隔离，而非仅依赖提示级防御。

Abstract: Safety-oriented instruction-following is supposed to keep LLM-controlled robots safe. We show it also creates an availability attack surface. By injecting short safety-plausible phrases (1-5 tokens) into a robots audio channel, an adversary can trigger the models safety reasoning to halt or disrupt execution without jailbreaking the model or overriding its policy. In the embodied setting, this is a semantic denial-of-service attack: the agent stops because the injected signal looks like a legitimate alert. Across four vision-language models, seven prompt-level defenses, three deployment modes, and single- and multi-injection settings, we find that prompt-only defenses trade off attack suppression against genuine hazard response. The strongest defenses reduce hard-stop attack success on some models, but defenses change the form of disruption, not its fact: suppressed hard stops re-emerge as acknowledge loops and false alerts, which we measure with Disruption Success Rate (DSR). We further find that injection variety is consistently more effective than repeating the same phrase, suggesting that models treat diverse safety cues as corroborating evidence. The practical implication is architectural rather than prompt-level: systems that route unauthenticated audio text directly into the LLM create an avoidable security dependency between safety monitoring and action selection.

</details>


### [29] [Network Impact of Post-Quantum Certificate Chain sizes on Time to First Byte in TLS Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24869)
*Matthew Chou,Phuong Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了后量子密码（PQC）在CDN环境下对TLS握手时间（TTFB）的影响，发现证书链大小超过传输层飞行限制会导致TTFB显著增加。实验对比了ECDSA和ML-DSA证书方案，并分析了会话恢复、证书大小优化和地理分布等CDN特性对延迟的缓解效果。研究表明，Merkle树证书（MTC）可将可支持的证书链大小提升2-3倍，优于CDN优化的约1.6倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算机的进展，传统X.509密码算法面临威胁，PQC部署势在必行。然而PQC证书链大小显著增加（5倍到20倍以上），会给互联网基础设施带来延迟成本。本文旨在量化CDN环境下向量子安全证书迁移的延迟代价，并探索缓解方案。

Method: 通过CDN聚焦的TLS条件评估首字节时间（TTFB），对比ECDCA和ML-DSA证书方案，通过控制证书扩展生成相似大小的证书链。利用Zeek在NCSA高性能计算系统上监控TLS流量以量化真实会话恢复率，并比较CDN驱动的大小优化与Merkle Tree证书（MTC）在飞行限制阈值下的表现。

Result: 证书链大小超过传输层数据飞行限制时TTFB出现离散增加。MTC可将可支持证书链大小提升2-3倍，而CDN优化仅支持约1.6倍提升。

Conclusion: 向PQC证书迁移会因证书链超限导致显著的TTFB延迟，Merkle Tree证书在缓解此问题方面优于传统CDN优化方案，能够更有效地支持更大的证书链。

Abstract: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is a rapidly growing deployment challenge as cryptographically relevant quantum computers (CRQC) continue to advance, leaving traditional cryptographic algorithms used in X.509 vulnerable to attack. However, PQC introduces significant deployment challenges in real-world networks, with handshake sizes increasing from 5x to over 20x compared to classical algorithms. In this work, we evaluate the time to first byte (TTFB) under CDN-focused TLS conditions to characterize the latency cost of transitioning existing internet infrastructure to quantum-safe certificate schemes. We observe discrete increases in TTFB as certificate chain sizes exceed transport layer data flight limits. To isolate the impact of certificate chains, we evaluate both ECDSA and ML-DSA-based certificate schemes, generating similarly sized certificate chains through controlled addition of certificate extensions. We additionally examine how CDN properties such as session resumption, certificate size optimizations, and geographical distribution reduce latency penalties. We utilize Zeek-monitored TLS traffic through a High-Performance Computing System (NCSA) with terabyte network connectivity across the nation to quantify real-world session resumption rates. We compare CDN-driven size optimization with Merkle Tree Certificates (MTC) to examine how size reductions allow certificate chains to remain under the flight limit threshold. We find that MTC allows for 2x-3x increase in supportable certificate chain size, whereas CDN-based optimizations yield more limited reductions, supporting up to approximately 1.6x certificate chain size increase.

</details>


### [30] [Verifying Provenance of Digital Media: Why the C2PA Specifications Fall Short](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24890)
*Enis Golaszewski,Neal Krawetz,Alan T. Sherman,Edward Zieglar,Sai K. Matukumalli,Roberto Yus,Carson L. Kegley,Michael Barthel,William Bowman,Bharg Barot,Kaur Kullman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对C2PA（内容来源与真实性联盟）系统进行了首次全面的独立安全分析，发现其当前规范未能实现声称的安全目标，也未达到可信部署所需的关键附加目标，可能误导用户和决策者，不建议在高风险场景中依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得大规模制造令人信服的虚假媒体变得容易，因此需要一个能够提供可验证数字内容来源的系统。本文旨在对已有的C2PA系统进行独立安全评估。

Method: 首次对C2PA核心协议进行了形式化方法分析，并进行了全面的独立安全评估。

Result: C2PA当前规范既未能实现其声称的安全目标，也未达到所有此类来源系统可信部署所需的关键附加目标。

Conclusion: C2PA是一个有前景的想法，但在用于金融披露、新闻业或法律证据等高风险的场景之前，不应过早依赖。

Abstract: The rapid rise of generative AI has made it easy to create convincing fake media at scale. In response, an industrial coalition has developed the Coalition for Content Provenance and Authenticity (C2PA), a system intended to provide verifiable provenance for digital content. Our research team conducted the first comprehensive, independent security analysis of C2PA. Our study includes the first formal-methods analysis of C2PA's core protocols. We find that the current C2PA specifications fail to achieve their claimed security goals. Furthermore, they also fail to achieve key additional goals, which all such provenance systems require for trustworthy deployment. As a result, C2PA may mislead users, platforms, and policymakers if relied upon prematurely. C2PA is a promising idea, but it should not yet be relied upon for high-stakes uses such as financial disclosures, journalism, or legal evidence.

</details>


### [31] [CAN-QA: A Question-Answering Benchmark for Reasoning over In-Vehicle CAN Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24935)
*Jing Chen,Abhijay Deevi,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAN-QA，首个将CAN总线流量分析重构为问答任务的基准数据集，包含33,128个QA对，并评估了LLM在该任务上的表现，发现其虽能捕捉统计规律但难以进行时间推理和多条件推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN入侵检测方法将问题视为分类任务，忽视了CAN流量的时间和关系结构，且与真实取证工作流中对流量的系统性推理需求不一致。

Method: 将原始CAN日志分割为时间窗口，利用确定性规则模板生成自然语言问题及其自动标注的答案，构建包含10个类别、33,128个QA对的基准数据集，并在True/False和多项选择格式下评估大语言模型。

Result: 大语言模型能捕捉表层统计规律，但在时间推理、多条件推断和高层行为语义理解上表现不佳。

Conclusion: CAN-QA为CAN流量分析提供了新的问答范式基准，揭示了现有LLM在该任务中的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The Controller Area Network (CAN) is a safety-critical in-vehicle communication protocol that lacks built-in security mechanisms, making intrusion detection essential. Existing approaches predominantly formulate CAN intrusion detection as a classification task, mapping complex traffic patterns to attack labels. However, this formulation abstracts away the temporal and relational structure of CAN traffic and misaligns with real-world forensic workflows, which require systematic reasoning about traffic behavior. To address this gap, we introduce CAN-QA, the first benchmark that reformulates CAN traffic analysis as a question-answering (QA) task. CAN-QA converts raw CAN logs into temporally segmented windows and applies deterministic rule-based templates to generate natural-language questions paired with automatically derived ground-truth answers. The resulting dataset comprises 33,128 QA pairs across 10 categories, each targeting distinct semantic and temporal properties of CAN traffic. Using CAN-QA, we evaluate large language models across both True/False and multiple-choice formats. Our results indicate that, although these models capture superficial statistical regularities, they struggle with temporal reasoning, multi-condition inference, and higher-level behavioral interpretation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Kriiiiss/CAN-QA.

</details>


### [32] [Extended Abstract: Shaperd: Easily Adoptable Real-Time Traffic Shaper for Fully Encrypted Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25069)
*Sarah Wilson,Stella Tian,Sina Kamali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Shaperd，一种实时流量整形工具，帮助全加密协议（FEPs）抵御基于被动流量分析和主动探测的审查封锁，通过新型约束系统对数据包内容进行实时整形，引入极小开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全加密协议（FEPs）虽被用于规避审查，但近年来审查者通过被动流量分析和主动探测等攻击手段能够有效封锁这些协议，亟需一种可轻松部署的防御方案。

Method: 设计Shaperd实时流量整形器，直接对数据包内容进行实时操作，使用新型约束系统允许用户生成具有任意期望特征的流量流。

Result: 初步结果表明Shaperd对底层系统的吞吐量引入极小开销。

Conclusion: Shaperd是一种易于采用且高效的实时流量整形方案，能有效帮助FEPs增强抗检测能力，且性能开销可控。

Abstract: Fully encrypted protocol-based tools (FEPs) are tools commonly used to circumvent censorship in restrictive regions, valued for their performance and security. However, in recent years, censors have been able to block them using an array of attacks based on passive traffic analysis and active probing. We propose Shaperd, an easily adoptable and real-time traffic shaper designed specifically to aid FEPs become more resilient to detection. Shaperd operates directly on packet contents in real time, using a novel constraint system to allow its users to generate traffic flows with any desired features. Our preliminary results reveal Shaperd introduces minimal overhead to the underlying system's throughput.

</details>


### [33] [Scalable Secure Biometric Authentication without Auxiliary Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25071)
*Alexander Bienstock,Daniel Escudero,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Zhen Zeng,Pranav Bhat,Ashok Singal,Prashant Sharma,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合人工智能与高级密码学技术的新型生物特征认证系统，在保证可扩展性和高性能的同时，提供了可证明的安全保障，以防止云端数据库泄露导致的大规模生物特征数据泄露。这是首个证明无需辅助标识符即可实现可扩展隐私保护生物特征认证的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于云端数据库的生物特征认证系统存在重大安全隐患：一旦数据库被攻破，所有用户的敏感生物特征数据可能全部泄露。而现有方案要么安全保护不足，要么因高计算开销而难以实际部署。

Method: 将人工智能与高级密码学技术以新颖方式结合，并在此过程中提供了多项优化。

Result: 提出的系统在提供可证明安全性（防止数据泄露）的同时，保持了可扩展性和高性能，是首个证明无需辅助标识符即可实现现实世界可扩展隐私保护生物特征认证的工作。

Conclusion: 该工作将推动生物特征认证的广泛工业应用及该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: The prevalence of biometric authentication has been on the rise due to its ease of use and elimination of weak passwords. To date, most biometric authentication systems have been designed for on-device authentication of the device owner (e.g., smartphones and laptops). Recently, biometric authentication systems have started to emerge that are designed to authenticate users against cloud databases storing representations of biometrics for large numbers of users (potentially millions), such as those facilitating biometric payments. However, the use of a large cloud database introduces a significant attack vector, as a breach of the database could lead to the compromise of all enrolled users' sensitive biometric data. Indeed, all such existing systems either do not adequately protect against such a breach, or are impractical to deploy and use due to their high computational overhead. In this work, we present a new biometric authentication system that provides provable security guarantees against data breaches, while remaining scalable and performant. To do so, we marry artificial intelligence with advanced cryptographic techniques in a novel fashion, providing several optimizations along the way. Our work is the first to show that real-world scalable privacy-preserving biometric authentication without auxiliary identifiers is feasible, and we believe that it will spur widespread industrial adoption and further research in this area.

</details>


### [34] [Structured Security Auditing and Robustness Enhancement for Untrusted Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25109)
*Lijia Lv,Xuehai Tang,Jie Wen,Jizhong Han,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGuard-Robust，一种针对Agent Skills包进行预加载审计的三路鲁棒分类框架，通过角色感知证据提取、选择性语义验证和一致性裁决，在多达404个包的评测中达到97.30%整体精确匹配和98.33%恶意风险召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护措施在语义保持改写下对恶意意图的恢复不一致，且包审计从单提示过滤升级为跨文件安全审查，需要更鲁棒的预加载审计方法。

Method: 将预加载审计形式化为三路鲁棒分类任务，提出SkillGuard-Robust框架，包含角色感知证据提取、选择性语义验证和一致性保持裁决三组件。

Result: 在404包留出聚合视图上达到97.30%整体精确匹配、98.33%恶意风险召回率和98.89%攻击精确一致性；在254包外部生态视图上分别达到99.66%、100.00%和100.00%。

Conclusion: 分解式包审计能实质性提升冻结模型和公共生态系统的鲁棒性，但更严苛的外部源迁移仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Agent Skills package SKILL.md files, scripts, reference documents, and repository context into reusable capability units, turning pre-load auditing from single-prompt filtering into cross-file security review. Existing guardrails often flag risk but recover malicious intent inconsistently under semantics-preserving rewrites. This paper formulates pre-load auditing for untrusted Agent Skills as a robust three-way classification task and introduces SkillGuard-Robust, which combines role-aware evidence extraction, selective semantic verification, and consistency-preserving adjudication. We evaluate SkillGuard-Robust on SkillGuardBench and two public-ecosystem extensions through five large evaluation views ranging from 254 to 404 packages. On the 404-package held-out aggregate, SkillGuard-Robust reaches 97.30% overall exact match, 98.33% malicious-risk recall, and 98.89% attack exact consistency. On the 254-package external-ecosystem view, it reaches 99.66%, 100.00%, and 100.00%, respectively. These results support a bounded conclusion: factorized package auditing materially improves frozen and public-ecosystem robustness, while harsher external-source transfer remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [35] [MGTEVAL: An Interactive Platform for Systemtic Evaluation of Machine-Generated Text Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25152)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Chengzhengxu Li,Zhaohan Zhang,Chenxu Zhao,Zepu Ruan,Chao Shen,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MGTEVAL是一个用于系统评估机器生成文本检测器的可扩展平台，整合了数据集构建、攻击、检测器训练和性能评估四大模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MGT检测评估面临数据集、预处理、攻击方式和评估指标碎片化的问题，导致结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 将评估流程组织为四个组件：数据集构建（使用可配置LLM生成MGT）、数据集攻击（支持12种文本攻击）、检测器训练（统一接口）和性能评估（报告有效性、鲁棒性和效率），并提供CLI和Web双界面。

Result: 提供了一个无需代码重写即可进行用户友好实验的平台，支持自定义基准测试构建和全面性能报告。

Conclusion: MGTEVAL通过标准化和模块化设计，有望提升MGT检测器评估的可比较性和可复现性。

Abstract: We present MGTEVAL, an extensible platform for systematic evaluation of Machine-Generated Text (MGT) detectors. Despite rapid progress in MGT detection, existing evaluations are often fragmented across datasets, preprocessing, attacks, and metrics, making results hard to compare and reproduce. MGTEVAL organizes the workflow into four components: Dataset Building, Dataset Attack, Detector Training, and Performance Evaluation. It supports constructing custom benchmarks by generating MGT with configurable LLMs, applying 12 text attacks to test sets, training detectors via a unified interface, and reporting effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency. The platform provides both command-line and Web-based interfaces for user-friendly experimentation without code rewriting.

</details>


### [36] [Secure Conformance Checking using Token-based Replay and Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25190)
*Luis-Armando Rodríguez-Flores,Luciano García-Bañuelos,Abel Armas-Cervantes,Astrid-Monserrat Rivera-Partida*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于同态加密的令牌重放算法，用于在保护事件日志隐私的前提下实现跨组织的合规性检查，并通过合成日志验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合规性检查要求流程模型和事件日志同时可见，但现实场景中日志持有者可能希望保护敏感信息，同时仍需对另一方持有的模型进行合规性检查。

Method: 基于同态加密的令牌重放算法，在加密域中执行合规性检查操作，允许在不泄露原始日志内容的情况下完成模型与日志的比对。

Result: 使用合成日志进行实验评估，结果表明所提技术具有实用性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种安全的合规性检查方法，能在保护事件日志隐私的同时进行跨组织的流程一致性分析。

Abstract: Conformance checking, one of the main process mining operations, aims to identify discrepancies between a process model and an event log. The model represents the expected behaviour, whereas the event log represents the actual process behaviour as captured in information systems' records. Traditionally, the process model and the event log are both accessible to the business analyst performing the conformance checking. However, in some contexts the log's owner may want to protect critical or sensitive information in the log and still check its conformance with respect to a model belonging to another party. In this paper, we propose a secure approach to conformance checking based on the well-known token-based replay algorithm and homomorphic encryption. An evaluation is performed using a synthetic log, showing the practicality of the proposed technique.

</details>


### [37] [Making AI-Assisted Grant Evaluation Auditable without Exposing the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25200)
*Kemal Bicakci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于TEE（可信执行环境）的架构，通过远程证明技术实现AI辅助基金评审的可审计、可问责且防操纵，同时不暴露模型权重和评分规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构开始将大语言模型作为基金评审的决策支持工具，但面临一个治理难题：模型和评分标准不能暴露给申请人（防止优化投递），同时评审过程必须保持可审计、可争议和可问责。

Method: 基于TEE的架构，利用远程证明让外部验证者检查所使用的模型、评分规则、提示模板和输入表示，但不暴露模型权重、专利评分逻辑或中间推理结果。核心产物是"经证明的评估包"（attested evaluation bundle）：一个包含原始提交哈希、规范输入哈希、模型与规则度量、评估输出的已签名时间戳记录。此外还设计了文档规范化与净化层（canonicalization and sanitization layer），用于防御提示注入攻击。

Result: 远程证明不能证明评估是公平或科学正确的，但能使评估过程的部分环节变得可外部验证。

Conclusion: 该架构定位在机密AI推理、可审计AI审计、零知识机器学习、算法问责和AI辅助同行评审等交叉领域，提供了一种实用且审慎的解决方案。

Abstract: Public agencies are beginning to consider large language models (LLMs) as decision-support tools for grant evaluation. This creates a practical governance problem: the model and scoring rubric should not be exposed in a way that allows applicants to optimize against them, yet the evaluation process must remain auditable, contestable, and accountable.
  We propose a TEE-based architecture that helps reconcile these requirements through remote attestation. The architecture allows an external verifier to check which model, rubric, prompt template, and input representation were used, without exposing model weights, proprietary scoring logic, or intermediate reasoning to applicants or infrastructure operators. The main artifact is an attested evaluation bundle: a signed, timestamped record linking the original submission hash, the canonical input hash, the model-and-rubric measurement, and the evaluation output.
  The paper also considers a scenario-specific prompt injection risk: applicant-controlled documents may contain hidden or indirect instructions intended to influence the LLM evaluator. We therefore include a canonicalization and sanitization layer that normalizes document representations and records suspicious transformations before inference. We position the design relative to confidential AI inference, attestable AI audits, zero-knowledge machine learning, algorithmic accountability, and AI-assisted peer review. The resulting claim is deliberately narrow: remote attestation does not prove that an evaluation is fair or scientifically correct, but it can make part of the evaluation process externally verifiable.

</details>


### [38] [MARD: A Multi-Agent Framework for Robust Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25264)
*Xueying Zeng,Youquan Xian,Sihao Liu,Xudong Mou,Yanze Li,Lei Cui,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MARD，一种基于多智能体框架的Android恶意软件检测方法，利用LLM编排决策过程并结合传统静态分析工具，在无需微调的情况下实现93.46%的F1分数，单次分析成本低于0.10美元，并能有效应对概念漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于机器学习的Android恶意软件检测模型面临概念漂移问题，且受限于浅层特征，缺乏深层语义理解和决策可解释性。虽然LLM具有语义推理能力，但直接处理原始代码会产生高昂的token开销，且无法充分释放LLM在复杂上下文中的深层逻辑推理潜力。

Method: 提出MARD多智能体框架，将底层确定性分析引擎作为按需执行工具，由LLM编排整个决策过程。基于ReAct范式设计自主多智能体交互机制，构建高可解释性的定罪证据链。

Result: 无需领域特定微调，MARD在F1分数上达到93.46%；单次复杂APK深度分析成本降至0.10美元以下；在跨越五年的评估中优于持续学习基线，表现出对概念漂移的鲁棒性和强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MARD有效弥合了LLM语义理解与传统静态分析之间的鸿沟，为Android恶意软件检测提供了一种高效、可解释且抗概念漂移的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of Android applications, traditional machine learning-based detection models suffer from concept drift. Additionally, they are constrained by shallow features, lacking deep semantic understanding and interpretability of decisions. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable semantic reasoning capabilities, directly processing massive raw code incurs prohibitive token overhead. Moreover, this approach fails to fully unleash the deep logical reasoning potential of LLMs within complex contexts. To address these limitations, we propose MARD, a multi-agent framework for robust Android malware detection. This framework effectively bridges the gap between the semantic understanding of LLMs and traditional static analysis. It treats underlying deterministic analysis engines as on-demand execution tools, while utilizing the LLM to orchestrate the entire decision-making process. By designing an autonomous multi-agent interaction mechanism based on the ReAct paradigm, MARD constructs a highly interpretable evidentiary chain for conviction. Furthermore, we radically reduce the total cost of conducting a deep analysis of a single complex APK to under $0.10. Evaluations demonstrate that, without any domain-specific fine-tuning, MARD achieves an F1 score of 93.46%. It not only outperforms continual learning baselines but also exhibits robustness against concept drift and strong cross-domain generalization capabilities in evaluations spanning up to five years.

</details>


### [39] [ReTokSync: Self-Synchronizing Tokenization Disambiguation for Generative Linguistic Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25486)
*Yaofei Wang,Rui Wang,Weilong Pang,JiaLiang Han,Yuan Qi,Donghui Hu,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTokSync，一种针对生成式语言隐写中分词歧义问题的自同步消歧框架，通过监控接收端视角的分词状态并在歧义发生时触发校正复位，将歧义影响限制为稀疏残差比特错误而非全局失同步，在保持安全性、文本质量和嵌入容量的同时实现99.7%以上的提取精度，并结合辅助通道实现100%端到端恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语言隐写在实用部署中面临分词歧义攻击：同一表面文本在接收端可能被重新分词为不同的token序列，导致编解码双方共享的解码状态断裂，单个局部不匹配会传播为完全提取失败。现有方案要么移除歧义token（扭曲生成分布、损害安全性），要么保持分布但大幅降低嵌入容量或引入过高运行时开销。

Method: 提出ReTokSync（重分词同步）自同步消歧框架：在生成过程中实时监控接收端视角的分词结果，仅在歧义实际发生时触发校正复位操作，将分词歧义的影响限制为稀疏的残差比特错误，而非全局失同步。该方法保持无歧义位置完全不受干扰，且与底层隐写算法兼容。

Result: 在英文和中文设置下的实验表明，ReTokSync在分布安全性（零KL散度）、文本质量、嵌入容量和运行时开销方面最接近隐写基线，同时提取准确率超过99.7%。进一步结合辅助通道实现双通道隐蔽通信，在所有评估配置下达到100%端到端恢复。

Conclusion: ReTokSync有效解决了分词歧义导致的全局失同步问题，以最小代价实现了高精度提取，并可通过双通道机制实现完美恢复，为生成式语言隐写的实用部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative linguistic steganography (GLS) enables covert communication by embedding secret messages into the natural language generation process. In practical deployment, however, GLS is vulnerable to tokenization ambiguity: the same surface text may be re-tokenized into a different token sequence at the receiver, breaking the shared decoding state between the communicating parties so that a single local mismatch can propagate into complete extraction failure. Existing solutions either remove ambiguous tokens -- distorting the generation distribution and compromising security -- or preserve the distribution at the cost of substantially reduced embedding capacity or prohibitive runtime overhead. To address this issue, we propose ReTokSync (Re-Tokenization Synchronization), a self-synchronizing disambiguation framework that monitors the receiver-view tokenization during generation and triggers a corrective reset only when ambiguity actually occurs. By confining the effect of tokenization ambiguity to sparse residual bit errors rather than global desynchronization, ReTokSync leaves ambiguity-free positions entirely untouched and remains compatible with the underlying steganographic algorithm. Experiments on both English and Chinese settings show that ReTokSync stays closest to the steganographic baseline in distributional security (zero KL divergence), text quality, embedding capacity, and runtime, while achieving extraction accuracy above 99.7\%. Building on this property, we further develop a two-channel covert communication mechanism in which ReTokSync serves as the primary channel and a reliable auxiliary channel corrects the remaining errors, achieving 100\% end-to-end recovery across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [40] [Medoid Prototype Alignment for Cross-Plant Unknown Attack Detection in Industrial Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25544)
*Luyao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于中心点原型对齐的跨工厂未知攻击检测框架，通过将异构流量压缩到可比较的表示空间、提取鲁棒的中心点原型并进行原型校准迁移，解决了工业控制系统流量高度站点依赖、标签稀缺和未知攻击检测难题。在天然气和水储存控制系统上的实验表明，该方法在四个未知攻击迁移任务中平均准确率达到0.843，平均F1分数达到0.838，优于所有对比模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工控入侵检测模型难以跨工厂部署，原因在于ICS流量高度依赖于具体站点，标签稀缺，且部署后常出现未见过的未知攻击。直接对齐源域和目标域样本存在噪声大、迁移稳定性差的问题。

Method: 提出中心点原型对齐框架：1) 将异构流量压缩到可比较的表示空间；2) 提取鲁棒的中心点原型以总结各领域的局部操作结构；3) 设计原型校准迁移目标，对齐目标原型与源原型，同时保持源域判别性并鼓励目标域置信预测。

Result: 在天然气和水储存控制系统上的实验表明，该方法在四个未知攻击迁移任务中平均准确率达0.843，平均F1分数达0.838，优于所有对比模型。分析还显示源-目标方向间存在明显的迁移不对称性，原型引导在具有挑战性的反向迁移设置中尤其有效。

Conclusion: 中心点原型对齐是应对域偏移下鲁棒工业入侵检测的实用解决方案，可有效降低跨域噪声匹配，提升异构工业条件下的迁移稳定性。

Abstract: Deploying an intrusion detector trained in one industrial plant to another remains difficult because Industrial Control System (ICS) traffic is highly site-dependent, labels are scarce, and unseen attacks often appear after deployment. To address this challenge, this paper introduces a medoid prototype alignment framework for cross-plant unknown attack detection. Instead of aligning all source and target samples directly, the method first compresses heterogeneous traffic into a comparable representation space and then extracts robust medoid prototypes that summarize local operational structure in each domain. A prototype-calibrated transfer objective is further designed to align target prototypes with source prototypes while preserving source-domain discrimination and encouraging confident target predictions. This strategy reduces noisy cross-domain matching and improves transfer stability under heterogeneous industrial conditions. Experiments conducted on natural gas and water storage control systems show that the proposed method achieves the best average performance among all compared models, reaching an average accuracy of 0.843 and an average F1-score of 0.838 across four unknown-attack transfer tasks. The analysis also shows clear transfer asymmetry between source-target directions and confirms that prototype guidance is especially helpful on challenging reverse-transfer settings. These findings suggest that medoid prototype alignment is a practical solution for robust industrial intrusion detection under domain shift.

</details>


### [41] [SnapGuard: Lightweight Prompt Injection Detection for Screenshot-Based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25562)
*Mengyao Du,Han Fang,Haokai Ma,Jiahao Chen,Kai Xu,Quanjun Yin,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SnapGuard是一种轻量级的截图级网页智能体提示注入检测方法，通过分析截图中的视觉稳定性和对比度反转恢复的文本信号，在不使用大型视觉语言模型的情况下，实现了比GPT-4o更快的检测速度（1.81秒 vs 14.50秒）和0.75的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于截图的网页智能体容易受到提示注入攻击，而现有基于文本的防御方法无效。多模态检测方法依赖大型视觉语言模型（VLM），计算开销大（推理时间和GPU内存）。因此，需要一种轻量级的截图级提示注入检测方法。

Method: SnapGuard将提示注入检测重构为多模态表示分析：1）视觉稳定性指标，检测恶意内容导致的异常平滑梯度分布；2）通过对比度极性反转恢复的面向动作的文本信号。利用这两种互补信号进行轻量级检测。

Result: 在8种攻击和2种良性设置下，SnapGuard达到0.75的F1分数，性能优于GPT-4o-prompt，速度快8倍（1.81秒 vs 14.50秒），且不引入额外内存开销。

Conclusion: SnapGuard证明了通过分析截图中的视觉和文本特征进行轻量级提示注入检测是可行的，为基于截图的网页智能体提供了一种高效且准确的防御方案。

Abstract: Web agents have emerged as an effective paradigm for automating interactions with complex web environments, yet remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that embed malicious instructions into webpage content to induce unintended actions. This threat is further amplified for screenshot-based web agents, which operate on rendered visual webpages rather than structured textual representations, making predominant text-centric defenses ineffective. Although multimodal detection methods have been explored, they often rely on large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring significant computational overhead. The bottleneck lies in the complexity of modern webpages: VLMs must comprehend the global semantics of an entire page, resulting in substantial inference time and GPU memory usage. This raises a critical question: can we detect prompt injection attacks from screenshots in a lightweight manner? In this paper, we observe that injected webpages exhibit distinct characteristics compared to benign ones from both visual and textual perspectives. Building on this insight, we propose SnapGuard, a lightweight yet accurate method that reformulates prompt injection detection as multimodal representation analysis over webpage screenshots. SnapGuard leverages two complementary signals: a visual stability indicator that identifies abnormally smooth gradient distributions induced by malicious content, and action-oriented textual signals recovered via contrast-polarity reversal. Extensive evaluations across eight attacks and two benign settings demonstrate that SnapGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.75, outperforming GPT-4o-prompt while being 8x faster (1.81s vs. 14.50s) and introducing no additional memory overhead.

</details>


### [42] [The Surprising Universality of LLM Outputs: A Real-Time Verification Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25634)
*Alex Bogdan,Adrian de Valois-Franklin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 发现前沿LLM的输出token秩频分布统一服从Mandelbrot分布（而非Zipf），基于此提出一种仅需CPU、每token 2.6微秒的评分原语，可实现模型指纹识别和黑盒输出评估，延迟比现有基于采样的检测器低五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的文本检测器延迟高、计算成本大，无法满足大规模或实时场景的需求。同时，缺乏一种无需密码学水印或访问模型内部即可验证模型来源的轻量级方法。

Method: 对6个前沿LLM（来自5个供应商）的输出进行统计建模，发现token秩频分布统一服从两参数Mandelbrot分布（而非Zipf），拟合优度R²>0.94（34/36）。基于该分布作为模型无关的参考分布，设计一种轻量级评分原语：可利用模型log概率（若有）或仅用秩信息（对封闭API），实现单次前向评估。

Result: 评分原语延迟为2.6微秒/token（CPU），比采样检测器低约100,000倍。模型间的Mandelbrot参数q（1.63-3.69）差异远超模型内bootstrap标准差（0.03-0.10），实现数十个标准差的分离。在FRANK、TruthfulQA、HaluEval上的初步实验表明，该原语在词汇异常、不支持的实体检测上有效，但对领域内词汇的推理错误无效。

Conclusion: 提出了一种极低延迟的LLM输出评估原语，具备统计模型指纹识别和黑盒评估双重能力，适合作为复合评估栈中的首层筛选工具，而非替代基于采样或基于源条件的验证器。

Abstract: We report a striking statistical regularity in frontier LLM outputs that enables a CPU-only scoring primitive running
  at 2.6 microseconds per token, with estimated latency up to 100,000$\times$ (five orders of magnitude) below existing
  sampling-based detectors. Across six contemporary models from five independent vendors, two generation sizes, and five
  held-out domains, token rank-frequency distributions converge to the same two-parameter Mandelbrot ranking
  distribution, with 34 of 36 model-by-domain fits exceeding $R^{2} = 0.94$ and 35 of 36 favoring Mandelbrot over Zipf
  by AIC. The shared family does not collapse the models into statistical duplicates. Fitted Mandelbrot parameters
  remain cleanly separable between models: the cross-model spread in $q$ (1.63 to 3.69) exceeds its per-model bootstrap
  standard deviation (0.03 to 0.10) by more than an order of magnitude, yielding tens of standard deviations of
  separation per few thousand output tokens. Two capabilities follow. First, statistical model fingerprinting: text from
  a vendor-delivered LLM can be tested against its claimed model family without cryptographic watermarks or access to
  model internals, supporting provenance verification and silent-substitution audits. Second, a model-agnostic reference
  distribution for black-box output assessment, from which we derive a single-pass scoring primitive that composes with
  model log probabilities when available and degrades to a rank-only mode usable on closed APIs. Pilot results on
  FRANK, TruthfulQA, and HaluEval map where the primitive helps (lexical anomalies, unsupported entities) and where it
  structurally cannot (reasoning errors in domain-appropriate vocabulary). We position the primitive as a first-pass
  triage layer in compound evaluation stacks, not as a replacement for sampling-based or source-conditioned verifiers.

</details>


### [43] [Towards Agentic Investigation of Security Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25846)
*Even Eilertsen,Vasileios Mavroeidis,Gudmund Grov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的实验性智能体工作流，通过预定义查询和受限工具访问（Suricata日志的结构化SQL查询和基于grep的文本搜索），自动化安全告警调查的早期阶段。该工作流能够查询数据概览、基于概览结果选择查询、提取原始证据并给出最终判定。实验表明，该工作流生成的判定准确率显著高于直接使用LLM的基线方法，有效减少了分析师的手动工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师被大量告警淹没，且多数检测系统提供的上下文信息不足。早期调查通常需要跨多个日志源进行手动关联，耗时且低效。

Method: 构建了一个智能体工作流，包含：（1）预定义查询模块提供数据概览；（2）LLM组件基于概览结果选择查询工具（Suricata日志的结构化SQL和grep文本搜索）；（3）从查询结果中提取原始证据；（4）生成最终告警判定。该方法将分析师的现有调查实践与LLM的结构化使用相结合，克服了直接对高容量非结构化数据应用LLM的局限性。

Result: LLM驱动的智能体工作流在告警调查的准确率上显著高于直接使用相同LLM（无该工作流）产生的判定结果。

Conclusion: 通过结合现有人工分析师的调查实践与结构化方法，将LLM作为虚拟安全分析师使用，可以有效辅助并减少手动工作负荷。

Abstract: Security analysts are overwhelmed by the volume of alerts and the low context provided by many detection systems. Early-stage investigations typically require manual correlation across multiple log sources, a task that is usually time-consuming. In this paper, we present an experimental, agentic workflow that leverages large language models (LLMs) augmented with predefined queries and constrained tool access (structured SQL over Suricata logs and grep-based text search) to automate the first stages of alert investigation. The proposed workflow integrates queries to provide an overview of the available data, and LLM components that selects which queries to use based on the overview results, extracts raw evidence from the query results, and delivers a final verdict of the alert. Our results demonstrate that the LLM-powered workflow can investigate log sources, plan an investigation, and produce a final verdict that has a significantly higher accuracy than a verdict produced by the same LLM without the proposed workflow. By recognizing the inherent limitations of directly applying LLMs to high-volume and unstructured data, we propose combining existing investigation practices of real-world analysts with a structured approach to leverage LLMs as virtual security analysts, thereby assisting and reducing the manual workload.

</details>


### [44] [Prime-Field PINI: Machine-Checked Composition Theorems for Post-Quantum NTT Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25878)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对素数域上的算术掩码首次进行了机器验证的组合定理证明，揭示了流水线级间无新鲜掩码会导致中间线非均匀从而易受差分功耗分析攻击，并通过Lean 4形式化验证了18个定理，诊断了Microsoft Adams Bridge PQC加速器中的架构缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔掩码的组合理论（NI、SNI、PINI）已成熟，但基于素数域$\mathbb{Z}_q$的算术掩码——NTT后量子密码的基础——一直缺乏类似的形式化组合理论。

Method: 提出"renewal argument"：两级流水线间施加新鲜随机掩码后，中间线完全均匀，与第一级安全参数无关；推导出PF-PINI组合定理（无新鲜掩码时中间线多重性达$k_1$），并形式化Barrett归约的代数模型与硬件忠实模型之间的桥梁。

Result: 1）证明两级PF-PINI gadget组合在新鲜掩码下满足PF-PINI($k_2$)，级1的多重性被完全擦除；2）无新鲜掩码时中间线多重性为$k_1$；3）在Lean 4中完成18个机器检查的证明，零sorry存根；4）形式化诊断Adams Bridge加速器：缺乏级间新鲜掩码导致Barrett输出线在一阶探测模型下非均匀。

Conclusion: 计算证据进一步表明"1-Bit Barrier"在Barrett和Montgomery归约中具有普遍性，为后量子密码掩码硬件设计提供了形式化验证基础。

Abstract: This is Paper 6 of a series of formally-verified analyses of masked NTT hardware for post-quantum cryptography; Paper 1 [1] established structural dependency analysis of the QANARY platform, and Paper 2 [2] quantified security margins under partial NTT masking. Boolean masking composition is well-understood through NI, SNI, and PINI. Arithmetic masking over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for prime $q$, the foundation of NTT-based post-quantum cryptography, has lacked an analogous theory. We prove, to our knowledge, the first machine-checked composition theorems for arithmetic masking over prime fields. Our key insight is the renewal argument: when a fresh random mask is applied between two pipeline stages, the intermediate wire becomes perfectly uniform regardless of Stage 1's security parameter. For two PF-PINI gadgets with parameters $k_1$ and $k_2$, the composed two-stage pipeline with fresh masking satisfies PF-PINI($k_2$), Stage 1's multiplicity is completely erased from the composed output. Without fresh masking, intermediate wires have multiplicity up to $k_1$, creating a necessary condition for differential power analysis. We formalize both theorems in Lean 4 with 18 machine-checked proofs and zero sorry stubs. We formally bridge the algebraic and hardware-faithful arithmetic models of Barrett reduction, and instantiate the theorems to formally diagnose Microsoft's Adams Bridge PQC accelerator: its absence of fresh inter-stage masking leaves Barrett output wires non-uniform under the first-order probing model, the same architectural flaw that two independent empirical analyses [3, 4] and our own prior structural analysis [1] identified. Computational evidence further suggests the 1-Bit Barrier is universal across Barrett and Montgomery reductions.

</details>

### [45] [Formalizing the Real Numbers in Homotopy Type Theory with Cubical Agda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24782)
*Jackson Brough*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在 Cubical Agda 中形式化了 HoTT 书中基于高阶归纳-归纳类型族的柯西实数构造，避免了经典构造中可数选择、集合包（setoid）簿记负担和谓词类型理论中戴德金分割的宇宙层级追踪问题，无需公设或漏洞即可类型检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造数学中的实数构造一直存在妥协：经典柯西完备性证明需要可数选择公理；Bishop 的集合包（setoid）构造在每个定义和定理上引入持续的簿记开销；谓词类型理论中的戴德金分割强制带来麻烦的宇宙层级追踪。Homotopy Type Theory (HoTT) 书提出了一种避免上述三种妥协的替代构造。

Method: 在 Cubical Agda 证明助手中形式化 HoTT 书中提出的柯西实数构造，该构造以高阶归纳-归纳类型族（higher inductive-inductive type family）定义实数。Cubical Agda 原生支持高阶归纳类型，使得该构造可以直接表达。

Result: 代码无需公设（postulates）或漏洞（holes）即可通过类型检查，为后续的构造分析机器辅助工作提供了基础。

Conclusion: HoTT 书中的柯西实数构造可以在 Cubical Agda 中成功形式化，且避免了经典构造中的妥协，为构造性分析的形式化研究奠定了可行基础。

Abstract: Real numbers in constructive mathematics have always seemed to require compromises of one form or another. Classical proofs of Cauchy completeness require countable choice, Bishop's setoid construction introduces persistent bookkeeping overhead on every definition and theorem, and Dedekind cuts force cumbersome universe-level tracking in predicative type theory. The Homotopy Type Theory (HoTT) book presents an alternative construction of the Cauchy real numbers as a higher inductive-inductive type family, avoiding all three compromises. We formalize the HoTT book reals in Cubical Agda, a proof assistant whose native support for higher inductive types allows the construction to be expressed directly. The code type-checks without postulates or holes, providing a foundation for further machine-assisted work in constructive analysis.

</details>


### [46] [Logic of Fuzzy Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24907)
*Kush Grover,Pratham Gupta,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模糊时变信号约束的新型时间逻辑，以路径为基本对象，分离几何与逻辑关注点，用于运动规划中的规范表达，具有更简洁的公式、更细致的满意度度量，并支持从示范中学习规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信号时序逻辑（STL）在机器人运动规划中虽被广泛使用，但其将几何与逻辑混为一谈，导致公式复杂且难以反映行为偏好；此外，STL对人类直接编写规范不够友好，也不易于从示范数据中学习规范。

Method: 以路径作为一等公民，基于模糊时变信号约束构建新型时间逻辑，将几何与逻辑分离；定义更精细的满意度概念以反映行为偏好；并设计相应的学习算法，给出原型实现。

Result: 通过多个场景示例展示了新逻辑在规范表达上的简洁性、灵活性和可理解性优势；给出了学习算法的原型实现，并讨论了模型检验与监控的可能性。

Conclusion: 新逻辑相比STL更适用于人类给定的运动规划规范表达，也更适合从示范中学习规范，在传统验证和数据驱动的任务学习方面均有优势。

Abstract: We introduce a new family of temporal logics intended for specifications in motion planning (MP). It builds upon the signal temporal logic (STL), which is a linear-time logic over real-valued signals that possess quantitative semantics and thus became popular in the areas of cyber-physical systems, robotics, and specifically robot MP. However, in contrast to STL, the proposed logic works with paths as first-class citizens, separating the concerns of geometry and of logic. This in turn leads to simpler and more understandable formulae, and a more refined notion of satisfaction being able to reflect also preferences over behaviours. Technically, the logic is built on fuzzy, time-varying signal constraints. As a consequence of this expressivity, it is (i) more usable for human-given specifications in MP and (ii) more amenable to learning specifications from demonstrations than other logics. The former is important for the traditional style of verification in robot MP; the latter is becoming recognized as crucial for mining data-given tasks and controller synthesis in human-aware MP. We expose the advantages of our proposed logic on examples and show the versatility and flexibility of the framework on a number of scenarios. Finally, we give a learning algorithm with a prototype implementation and discuss the possibilities of model checking and monitoring.

</details>

*Mayuko Kori,Kazuki Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个余代数框架，用于将部分可观测系统（如POMDP）转化为完全可观测的信念系统，并证明了两者的语义等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信念构造是将部分可观测系统转化为完全可观测系统的核心技术，在人工智能和形式化验证中至关重要，但缺乏统一的范畴论框架。

Method: 将单子提升到切片范畴，引入信念分解来根据观测重组状态，并将其与Silva等人的余代数确定性化方法结合，形成余代数化的信念构造。

Result: 证明了部分可观测系统的语义与对应的信念余代数的语义一致；进一步给出了后者与完全可观测对应系统语义一致的条件。

Conclusion: 恢复了POMDP与信念MDP之间的标准等价性，并为带多重集单子的加权转移系统得到了新的等价性结果。

Abstract: The belief construction is a fundamental technique for transforming partially observable systems to fully observable ones while preserving the relevant semantics. It plays a central role in the analysis of partially observable systems, in particular partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs), which is a central model in artificial intelligence and formal verification. In this paper, we develop a coalgebraic framework for the belief construction. To handle observations categorically, we lift a monad to slice categories and introduce a belief decomposition that reorganizes states according to their observations. This allows us to introduce a coalgebraic generalization of the belief construction, obtained by combining the belief decomposition with the coalgebraic determinization of Silva, Bonchi, Bonsangue, and Rutten. In this framework, we show that the semantics of a partially observable system coincides with that of the corresponding belief coalgebra. We then study when the latter further agrees with the semantics of its fully observable counterpart, and use this to identify conditions under which the semantics of a partially observable system coincides with that of the corresponding fully observable belief system. As consequences, we recover the standard equivalence between POMDPs and belief MDPs, and obtain a new equivalence result for weighted transition systems with the multiset monad.

</details>

*Matteo Acclavio,Lutz Straßburger,Vladimir Zamdzhiev*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于原子流（一种特殊形式的字符串图）定义了BV逻辑的证明同一性概念，并在此基础上强化了BV-范畴的概念，证明了该范畴相对于BV逻辑是可靠的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BV-范畴旨在为BV逻辑的证明提供范畴语义，但由于缺乏一致性定理和明确的证明同一性概念，两者之间的精确关系尚不清晰。本文旨在改善这一状况。

Method: 基于原子流（一种特殊形式的字符串图）定义BV逻辑的证明同一性概念，并在该基础上强化现有的BV-范畴概念。

Conclusion: 证明了强化后的BV-范畴相对于BV逻辑是可靠的，从而厘清了BV-范畴与BV逻辑之间的精确关系。

Abstract: BV-categories are a recent development that aims to give categorical semantics to proofs in the logic BV. However, due to the absence of a coherence theorem on one side and a well-defined notion of proof identity for BV on the other side, the precise relation between BV-categories and the logic BV is still not clear. To improve on this situation, we define in this paper a notion of proof identity for BV, based on the notion of atomic flows, which can be seen as a special form of string diagrams. Based on this notion of proof identity, we then strengthen the existing notion of BV-category and prove that it is sound with respect to the logic.

</details>

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了描述逻辑S中部分有限模型下的查询蕴含问题，即给定知识库、查询和区分概念，判断在所有将区分概念解释为有限集的模型中查询是否成立。作者证明该问题对于合取查询是2-exptime完全的，并通过无穷模型手术技术推广了有限和无限情形下的已有结果。作为应用，展示了如何通过归约到部分有限查询蕴含来解决封闭谓词下的查询包含问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调有限模型推理与无限模型推理，研究部分有限模型——即推理任务附带一个公式指定模型中必须有限的部分。在描述逻辑中，有限模型和无限模型下的查询蕴含结果可能不同，需要统一处理框架。

Method: 以描述逻辑S（ALC扩展传递角色）为研究对象，提出部分有限查询蕴含问题。采用无穷模型手术技术，推广有限情形和无限情形下的论证方法，证明合取查询的部分有限蕴含问题复杂度为2-exptime。

Result: 证明了对于S描述逻辑，合取查询的部分有限蕴含问题是2-exptime可判定的，并且该结果推广了已有的有限和无限情形下的结论。

Conclusion: 部分有限模型推理为统一有限和无限模型推理提供了新框架，且可通过归约解决封闭谓词下的查询包含问题。

Abstract: Aiming to harmonise finite and infinite model reasoning, we initiate the study of partially finite models, where the reasoning task comes with a formula that specifies a part of the model that must be finite. We focus on the problem of partially finite query entailment in description logics (DLs): given a knowledge base (KB), a query, and a distinguished concept, decide whether the query holds in all models of the KB that interpret the distinguished concept as a finite set. To break the ground, we work with the DL S, an extension of the basic DL ALC with transitive roles, which is one of the simplest cases where finite and infinite query entailment diverge. Generalising previous results on the finite and infinite cases, we show that also partially finite entailment of conjunctive queries is in 2-exptime for S. The solution involves sophisticated infinite model surgery and goes far beyond combining the arguments for the two special cases. As a direct application, we show how the problem of query containment in the presence of closed predicates can be solved by reduction to partially finite query entailment.

</details>

*Jessica Newman,Benjamin Plummer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究博弈反应式合成中位置性属性的性质，证明每个ω-正则位置性属性（在状态或边标记博弈图上）都可在线性时态逻辑中表达，给出了ω-正则属性成为位置性的充要条件，并识别了良好行为的子类。通过语言变体证明，没有一类ω-正则位置性属性能同时包含前缀独立属性并对布尔运算封闭。最后讨论了在交替时态逻辑中的影响，分离出几个具有可处理模型检验的片段并比较其表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于拥有一个可用的规范逻辑，使得可处理的（tractable）合成得到保证，从而在博弈反应式合成中研究位置性属性的性质。

Method: 通过理论分析，证明每个ω-正则位置性属性在LTL中可表达；给出ω-正则属性成为位置性的充要条件；使用语言变体（varieties of languages）证明不可同时包含前缀独立属性并对布尔运算封闭；分离ATL片段并比较表达力。

Result: 证明了每个ω-正则位置性属性（状态或边标记）可在LTL中表达；给出了位置性的充要条件；证明了任何ω-正则位置性属性类不能同时包含前缀独立属性并封闭于布尔运算；识别了ATL中几个具有可处理模型检验的片段。

Conclusion: 位置性属性与LTL表达力有紧密联系，且存在理论上的限制（无法同时满足前缀独立与布尔封闭）。这些结果对ATL的片段划分和模型检验复杂度有重要启示。

Abstract: We study positional properties in the context of game-based reactive synthesis. Our motivation stems from having a usable specification logic, for which tractable synthesis is guaranteed. We demonstrate that every $ω$-regular positional property (with respect to state- or edge-labelled game graphs), is expressible in linear-time temporal logic. Additionally, we provide some necessary and sufficient conditions for when an $ω$-regular property is positional, and identify well-behaved subclasses of $ω$-regular positional properties. Using varieties of languages, we prove that no class of $ω$-regular positional properties can simultaneously contain a prefix-independent property and be closed under Boolean operations. We conclude by discussing the implications on alternating-time temporal logic, where we isolate a few different fragments with tractable model checking, and compare the associated expressivity of such fragments.

</details>

### [51] [MotionBricks: Scalable Real-Time Motions with Modular Latent Generative Model and Smart Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24833)
*Tingwu Wang,Olivier Dionne,Michael De Ruyter,David Minor,Davis Rempe,Kaifeng Zhao,Mathis Petrovich,Ye Yuan,Chenran Li,Zhengyi Luo,Brian Robison,Xavier Blackwell,Bernardo Antoniazzi,Xue Bin Peng,Yuke Zhu,Simon Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionBricks 是一个大规模实时生成框架，通过模块化潜空间生成骨干和智能原语，实现了在实时约束下高质量、多模态（速度、风格、关键帧）的运动生成，支持350,000+运动片段，达到15,000 FPS吞吐量，并成功部署到人形机器人上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式运动合成方法在实时可扩展性和细粒度多模态控制方面存在不足，难以满足工业级应用需求——即实时生成大量运动技能并支持速度命令、风格选择和精确关键帧等多种控制信号。

Method: 提出大规模模块化潜空间生成骨干，用单一模型有效建模350,000+运动片段；引入智能原语，提供统一的鲁棒直观接口用于导航和物体交互创作，支持即插即用式应用设计。

Result: 在开源和专有数据集上实现最先进的运动质量，实时吞吐量达15,000 FPS、延迟2ms；在完整的生产级动画演示中覆盖多种风格的导航和物-场景交互；成功部署于Unitree G1人形机器人实现实时机器人控制。

Conclusion: MotionBricks 弥合了生成式运动合成研究与工业应用之间的差距，证明了其在动画和机器人控制领域的灵活性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite transformative advances in generative motion synthesis, real-time interactive motion control remains dominated by traditional techniques. In this work, we identify two key challenges in bridging research and production: 1) Real-time scalability: Industry applications demand real-time generation of a vast repertoire of motion skills, while generative methods exhibit significant degradation in quality and scalability under real-time computation constraints, and 2) Integration: Industry applications demand fine-grained multi-modal control involving velocity commands, style selection, and precise keyframes, a need largely unmet by existing text- or tag-driven models. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MotionBricks: a large-scale, real-time generative framework with a two-fold solution. First, we propose a large-scale modular latent generative backbone tailored for robust real-time motion generation, effectively modeling a dataset of over 350,000 motion clips with a single model. Second, we introduce smart primitives that provide a unified, robust, and intuitive interface for authoring both navigation and object interaction. Applications can be designed in a plug-and-play manner like assembling bricks without expert animation knowledge. Quantitatively, we show that MotionBricks produces state-of-the-art motion quality on open-source and proprietary datasets of various scales, while also achieving a real-time throughput of 15,000 FPS with 2ms latency. We demonstrate the flexibility and robustness of MotionBricks in a complete production-level animation demo, covering navigation and object-scene interaction across various styles with a unified model. To showcase our framework's application beyond animation, we deploy MotionBricks on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot to demonstrate its flexibility and generalization for real-time robotic control.

</details>


### [52] [VISION-SLS: Safe Perception-Based Control from Learned Visual Representations via System Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24894)
*Antoine P. Leeman,Shuyu Zhan,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISION-SLS是一种基于RGB图像的非线性输出反馈控制方法，通过预训练视觉特征的低维观测映射和系统级合成（SLS）优化策略，在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学条件下提供鲁棒的约束满足保证。在仿真和硬件实验中，该方法在大尺度视觉运动任务（4D车、10D四旋翼、59D人形机器人）上实现了安全、信息收集行为，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输出反馈控制方法在处理高分辨率RGB图像时面临可扩展性与安全性保证之间的权衡，尤其在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学条件下，缺乏既能保证约束满足又能扩展到高维视觉任务的方法。

Method: (i) 从预训练视觉特征中学习低维观测映射，并附带状态依赖误差界；(ii) 通过系统级合成（SLS）优化因果仿射时变输出反馈策略；(iii) 采用序列凸规划结合高效Riccati递归求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 在4D车、10D四旋翼（>=512x512像素）和59D部分可观测人形机器人任务上实现安全行为；在硬件平台上成功控制地面车辆，安全率和求解时间均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 学习型视觉抽象与高效求解器的结合使基于SLS的安全视觉运动输出反馈控制在规模化任务中变得实用。

Abstract: We propose VISION-SLS, a method for nonlinear output-feedback control from high-resolution RGB images which provides robust constraint satisfaction guarantees under calibrated uncertainty bounds despite partial observability, sensor noise, and nonlinear dynamics. To enable scalability while retaining guarantees, we propose: (i) a learned low-dimensional observation map from pretrained visual features with state-dependent error bounds, and (ii) a causal affine time-varying output-feedback policy optimized via System Level Synthesis (SLS). We develop a scalable, novel solver for the resulting nonconvex program that leverages sequential convex programming coupled with efficient Riccati recursions. On two simulated visuomotor tasks (a 4D car and a 10D quadrotor) with >= 512 x 512 pixels and a 59D humanoid task with partial observability, our method enables safe, information-gathering behavior that reduces uncertainty while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction with empirically-calibrated error bounds. We also validate our method on hardware, safely controlling a ground vehicle from onboard images, outperforming baselines in safety rate and solve times. Together, these results show that learned visual abstractions coupled with an efficient solver make SLS-based safe visuomotor output-feedback practical at scale. The code implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/VISION-SLS.

</details>


### [53] [An analysis of sensor selection for fruit picking with suction-based grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24906)
*Eva Krueger,Marcus Rosette,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人水果采摘中难以可靠检测采摘状态的问题，设计了一种集成到自适应吸盘式苹果夹爪中的多模态传感套件，通过阶段依赖的传感器评估和最小传感器集识别，实现了90%以上的采摘/滑落事件检测精度，且随机森林模型预测误差在0.09秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘常因无法可靠检测果实是否成功摘取而导致效率低、作物损伤高；现有方法多采用视觉或多传感器学习，但最小传感器集和阶段依赖的感知策略尚缺乏探索。

Method: 设计集成到自适应吸盘式苹果夹爪中的多模态传感套件，进行阶段依赖的传感器信息性评估，识别最小传感器集，并使用随机森林和多层感知器分类器进行采摘状态分类。

Result: 在真实果园实验中，随机森林和多层感知器分类器检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败准确率超过90%；随机森林预测采摘/滑落事件的时间误差在0.09秒以内。

Conclusion: 本文提出的阶段依赖多模态感知策略和最小传感器集方法可有效实现高精度、低延迟的采摘状态检测，为机器人水果采摘的可靠性和效率提升提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting often fails to reliably detect whether a fruit has been successfully picked, limiting efficiency and increasing crop damage. This problem is difficult due to compliant fruit and grippers, variable stem attachment, and occlusions in orchard environments. Prior work has explored vision-based perception and multi-sensor learning approaches for pick state estimation. However, minimal sensor sets and phase-dependent sensing strategies for accurate pick and slip detection remain largely unexplored. In this work, we design and evaluate a multimodal sensing suite integrated into a compliant suction-based apple gripper. Our approach is unique because it identifies which sensors are most informative at different phases of the pick, enabling predictive detection of failures before they occur. The contributions of this paper are a phase-dependent evaluation of multimodal sensors and the identification of minimal sensor sets for reliable pick state classification. Experiments in a real apple orchard show that Random Forest and Multilayer Perceptron classifiers detect successful picks and impending failures with over 90% accuracy, and Random Forest predicts pick/slip events within 0.09 s of human-annotated ground truth.

</details>

*Fang Wan,Guangyi Huang,Tianyu Wu,Zishang Zhang,Bangchao Huang,Haoran Sun,Mingdong Chen,Chaoyang Song*

TL;DR: 本文提出了asRoBallet，这是首个在仿人形球型机器人硬件上成功部署强化学习的系统。通过高保真MuJoCo仿真（显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚子机械结构）和摩擦感知强化学习框架，实现了零样本Sim2Real迁移，并设计了低成本硬件平台和iOS生态系统。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 构建高保真MuJoCo仿真，显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚子机械结构，捕捉寄生振动和接触不连续性；2) 提出摩擦感知强化学习框架，掌握轮-球和球-地界面的耦合滚动、横向和扭转摩擦通道，实现零样本Sim2Real迁移；3) 通过减法重构方式设计低成本硬件平台asRoBallet；4) 开发通用iOS生态系统，将消费电子产品转变为低延迟接口。

Result: 成功实现了仿人形球型机器人上强化学习的首次硬件部署，并完成了零样本Sim2Real迁移。
