Abstract: We introduce asRoBallet, to the best of our knowledge, the first successful deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) on a humanoid ballbot hardware. Historically, ballbots have served as a canonical benchmark for underactuated and nonholonomic control, which are characterized by a reality gap in complex friction models for wheel-sphere-ground interactions. While current literature demonstrates successful handling of 3D balancing with LQR and MPC, transitioning to actual hardware for a humanoid ballbot using RL is currently hindered by critical gaps in contact modeling, actuator latency & jitter, and safe hardware exploration, and safe hardware exploration. This study proposes a high-fidelity MuJoCo simulation that explicitly models the discrete roller mechanics of ETH-type omni-wheels, thereby capturing parasitic vibrations and contact discontinuities that are previously ignored. We also developed a Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that achieves zero-shot Sim2Real transfer by mastering the coupled rolling, lateral, and torsional friction channels at the wheel-sphere and sphere-ground interfaces. We designed asRoBallet through subtractive reconfiguration, repurposing key components from an overconstrained quadruped and integrating them into a newly designed structural frame to achieve a robust research platform at low cost. We also developed a generalized iOS ecosystem that transforms consumer electronics into a low-latency interface, enabling a single operator to orchestrate expressive humanoid maneuvers via intuitive natural motion.

</details>


### [55] [Libra-VLA: Achieving Learning Equilibrium via Asynchronous Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24921)
*Yifei Wei,Linqing Zhong,Yi Liu,Yuxiang Lu,Xindong He,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Libra-VLA提出了一种粗到精的双系统VLA架构，通过将操作动作分解为离散的宏观方向意图和连续的微观姿态对齐，并采用异步执行策略，在训练均衡点实现最佳性能，缩小了语义-执行鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用单一生成范式，直接将视觉-语言特征映射为高频电机指令，忽视了机器人操作中固有的层次结构（宏观方向到达和微观姿态对齐），导致语义-执行鸿沟扩大，且高频预测给高层语义接地带来沉重负担。

Method: 提出粗到精双系统VLA架构：语义规划器（Semantic Planner）预测离散动作令牌捕获宏观方向意图，动作精化器（Action Refiner）以粗意图为条件生成高频连续动作进行精确对齐。通过解耦学习复杂度实现训练均衡，并利用结构模块化实现异步执行策略。

Result: 实证分析发现性能随动作分解粒度呈倒U型曲线，在两个子系统学习难度均衡时达到峰值。异步设计提供了可扩展、鲁棒且响应迅速的开放世界操作方案。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦粗到精的层次结构达到训练均衡，同时利用结构模块化实现异步执行，为开放世界机器人操作提供了可扩展、鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding high-level semantic instructions into executable physical actions. However, prevailing approaches typically adopt a monolithic generation paradigm, directly mapping visual-linguistic features to high-frequency motor commands in a flat, non-hierarchical fashion. This strategy overlooks the inherent hierarchy of robotic manipulation, where complex actions can be naturally modeled in a Hybrid Action Space, decomposing into discrete macro-directional reaching and continuous micro-pose alignment, severely widening the semantic-actuation gap and imposing a heavy representational burden on grounding high-level semantics to continuous actions. To address this, we introduce Libra-VLA, a novel Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System VLA architecture. We explicitly decouple the learning complexity into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy to strike a training equilibrium, while simultaneously leveraging this structural modularity to implement an asynchronous execution strategy. The Semantic Planner predicts discrete action tokens capturing macro-directional intent, while the Action Refiner conditions on coarse intent to generate high-frequency continuous actions for precise alignment. Crucially, our empirical analysis reveals that performance follows an inverted-U curve relative to action decomposition granularity, peaking exactly when the learning difficulty is balanced between the two sub-systems. With the asynchronous design, our approach offers a scalable, robust, and responsive solution for open-world manipulation.

</details>


### [56] [TEACar: An Open-Source Autonomous Driving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24934)
*Zhongzheng Zhang,Maxwell Ruyle,Andrew Kappes,Tyler Ruble,William Shaoul,Dana Moreno,Jack Penn,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEACAR是一个模块化、可扩展的1/14-1/16比例自动驾驶实验平台，采用四层机械架构和ROS 2软件系统，通过CNN转向控制器验证了其在ITS研究中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型自动驾驶赛车平台存在模块化不足、集成复杂度高、可扩展性受限等问题，难以满足ITS中对真实硬件在环验证的需求。

Method: 设计四层解耦式机械结构（感知、计算、驱动、电源），结合硬件抽象层与ROS 2软件架构，构建1/14-1/16比例原型平台TEACAR。

Result: 基于三种CNN转向控制器定量评估了机械稳定性、结构特性和软件性能，测量了推理延迟、功耗和系统运行时间等指标。

Conclusion: TEACAR提供了一个可扩展、模块化、成本效益高的ITS研究、教育和开发测试平台。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) increasingly rely on vision-based perception and learning-based control, necessitating experimental platforms that support realistic hardware-in-the-loop validation. Small-scale platforms for autonomous racing offer a practical path to hardware validation, but often suffer from limited modularity, high integration complexity, or restricted extensibility. This paper presents TEACAR, a 1/14- to 1/16-scale autonomous driving platform designed with modular mechanical architecture, hardware abstraction, and ROS 2-based software. The system adopts a four-layer deck structure that physically decouples sensing, computation, actuation, and power subsystems, improving structural rigidity while simplifying reconfiguration. We constructed and comprehensively evaluated the prototype of TEACAR. Its mechanical stability, structural characteristics, and software performance were quantified based on three CNN-based steering controllers. Inference latency, power consumption, and system operating time were measured to evaluate computational capability and robustness. Our experiments demonstrated that TEACAR offers a scalable, modular, and cost-effective testbed for ITS research, education, and development. Our project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [57] [DiscreteRTC: Discrete Diffusion Policies are Natural Asynchronous Executors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25050)
*Pengcheng Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Chensheng Peng,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Chenfeng Xu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略的原生掩码修复能力实现异步执行，解决了连续流匹配RTC在物理AI动态任务中因推理时修正导致的延迟和次优问题，在仿真和真实动态操作任务中取得了更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI需要在环境持续变化的同时行动，同步执行器的帧间暂停对动态任务致命。现有RTC方法基于流匹配策略，其修复依赖推理时修正而非基础策略，导致预训练收益小、需要特定微调、启发式引导且增加延迟。

Method: 观察到离散扩散策略通过迭代去掩码生成动作，天然支持异步执行。提出DiscreteRTC，用原生去掩码替代外部修正，无需额外代码实现异步修复，且支持早停实现自适应引导和降低推理成本。

Result: 在动态模拟基准和真实动态操作任务中，DiscreteRTC相比连续RTC和其他基线取得更高成功率。推理计算量仅为从头生成动作的0.7倍，在真实动态抓取任务中成功率比流匹配RTC高50%。

Conclusion: DiscreteRTC实现更简单（零代码实现异步修复）、推理更快、执行效果更好，是一种更优的物理AI异步执行方案。

Abstract: Unlike chatbots, physical AI must act while the world keeps evolving. Therefore, the inter-chunk pause of synchronous executors are fatal for dynamic tasks regardless of how fast the inference is. Asynchronous execution -- thinking while acting -- is therefore a structural requirement, and real-time chunking (RTC) makes it viable by recasting chunk transitions as inpainting: freezing committed actions and consistently generating the remainder. However, RTC with flow-matching policy is structurally suboptimal: its inpainting comes from inference-time corrections rather than the base policy, yielding little pre-training benefit, specific fine-tuning, heuristic guidance, and extra computation that inflates the latency. In this work, we observe that discrete diffusion policies, which generate actions by iteratively unmasking, are natural asynchronous executors that resolve all limitations at once: they are fine-tuning free since inpainting is their native operation, while early stopping further provides adaptive guidance and reduces inference cost. We propose DiscreteRTC, which replaces external corrections with native unmasking, and show on dynamic simulated benchmarks and real-world dynamic manipulation tasks that it achieves higher success rates than continuous RTC and other baselines. In summary, DiscreteRTC is simpler to implement with 0 lines of code for async inpainting, faster at inference with only 0.7x computation compared with generating actions from scratch, and better at execution with 50% higher success rate in real-world dynamic pick task compared with flow-matching-based RTC. More visualizations are on https://outsider86.github.io/DiscreteRTCSite/.

</details>


### [58] [Slot-hopping Enabled Loiter Guidance and Automation for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25292)
*Pradeep J,Siddhardha Kedarisetty,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对固定翼无人机走廊的交通拥堵管理问题，在现有游荡车道框架基础上，提出一种半协同引导策略。该策略让进入车道的无人机首先尝试与现有空游荡时隙会合，若不可行则通过最少数量无人机协调跳槽创建空位，实现紧凑走廊内的最小化干扰插入。数值仿真验证了方法的可行性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决固定翼无人机走廊中交通拥堵管理问题，在现有游荡车道框架基础上进一步优化，以实现更紧凑的无人机走廊和更小干扰的插入操作。

Method: 提出半协同引导策略：进入车道的无人机首先在其速度范围内尝试与现有空游荡时隙会合；若直接插入不可行，则通过最小数量的游荡无人机执行协调跳槽，创建合适位置的空时隙。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 半协同引导策略能够在保持紧凑走廊的同时，实现固定翼无人机以最小干扰插入游荡车道，为无人机走廊交通管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of traffic congestion management in fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors by further developing a recently introduced loiter-lane framework. A semi-cooperative guidance strategy is developed for inserting fixed-wing UAVs into a loiter lane with minimal disruption to the UAVs already operating within it, while enabling a more compact fixed-wing UAV corridor. Building on the concepts of cooperative and non-disruptive loiter-lane insertion, the proposed strategy makes the incoming UAV first attempt, within its speed bounds, to rendezvous with an existing empty loiter slot. If direct insertion is infeasible, a minimal number of loitering UAVs perform coordinated slot hopping to create a suitably positioned empty slot. The feasibility and performance of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [59] [HANDFUL: Sequential Grasp-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation with Resource Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25126)
*Ethan Foong,Yunshuang Li,Hao Jiang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDFUL框架，通过资源感知抓取（保留手指用于后续操作）来解决灵巧手在序列化操作中的多技能连续执行问题，在仿真和真实LEAP手上验证了该方法相比贪心抓取策略的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作研究多聚焦于单物体、单技能任务，忽略了实际应用中机器人需要顺序执行多个操作技能、同时保持对已抓取物体控制的需求。为此需要探索在抓取时预留手指资源以支持后续子任务的操作策略。

Method: 提出HANDFUL框架：(1) 通过手指级接触奖励来鼓励探索资源感知抓取；(2) 基于课程学习的策略来为下游任务选择合适的抓取；(3) 构建HANDFUL-Bench仿真基准，包含推、拉、压等第二子任务。

Result: 仿真实验表明，优先考虑资源感知抓取相比贪心优化初始抓取的基线方法，在第二子任务的成功率和鲁棒性上均有显著提升，并在真实LEAP手上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 资源感知抓取规划是多功能灵巧操作的关键原则，该工作为序列化灵巧操作任务建立了新的基准和方法框架。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands offer rich opportunities for multifunctional manipulation, where a robot must execute multiple skills in sequence while maintaining control over previously grasped objects. Most prior work in dexterous manipulation focuses on single-object, single-skill tasks. In contrast, our insight is that many sequential tasks require resource-aware grasps that conserve fingers for future actions. In this paper, we study sequential grasp-conditioned dexterous manipulation, where a robot first grasps an object and then performs a second, distinct manipulation subtask while preserving the initial grasp. We introduce HANDFUL, a learning framework that models finger usage as a limited resource and encourages exploration of resource-aware grasps through finger-level contact rewards. These grasps are subsequently selected for downstream tasks via curriculum-based policy learning. We further propose HANDFUL-Bench, a simulation benchmark that introduces sequential dexterous manipulation tasks across multiple secondsubtask objectives, including pushing, pulling, and pressing, under a shared grasp-conditioned setup. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that prioritizing resource-aware grasps improves second-subtask success and robustness compared to a baseline that greedily optimizes the initial grasp before attempting the second subtask. We additionally validate our approach on a real dexterous LEAP hand. Together, this work establishes resource-aware grasp planning as a key principle for multifunctional dexterous manipulation. Supplementary material is available on our website: https://handful-dex.github.io.

</details>


### [60] [ANCHOR: A Physically Grounded Closed-Loop Framework for Robust Home-Service Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25323)
*Jinhao Jiang,Shengyu Fang,Sibo Zuo,Yujie Tang,Yirui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ANCHOR提出了一种物理锚定的闭环框架，通过可验证的几何锚定、可操作性感知的导航规划以及最小责任层分层恢复机制，解决了移动机器人在开放词汇家庭环境中长期任务执行时符号计划与物理世界不一致的问题，在60次真实机器人实验中任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动恢复率达71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇移动操作系统在真实家庭环境中面临符号计划与物理世界之间的执行不一致问题，表现为三个局限：依赖预扫描语义地图导致场景变化后不一致、导航终点未考虑下游操作可行性导致"到达但无法操作"、异常处理采用无差别全局重规划无法有效遏制局部错误。

Method: ANCHOR框架包含三个核心机制：(i)物理锚定任务规划——将符号谓词绑定到可观测的几何锚点并在每个动作后重新验证；(ii)可操作性感知的基座对齐——确保导航终点满足运动学可达性和局部碰撞可行性；(iii)最小责任层分层恢复——将故障定位到感知层、基座-机械臂协调层和执行层，防止级联重试。

Result: 在60次真实机器人实验中，ANCHOR将任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，并在扰动条件下达到71.4%的恢复率。

Conclusion: 明确的物理锚定和结构化的故障遏制对于鲁棒的移动操作至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in open-vocabulary mobile manipulation have brought robots into real domestic environments. In such settings, reliable long-horizon execution under open-set object references and frequent disturbances becomes essential. However, many failures persist. These are not caused by semantic misunderstanding but by inconsistencies between symbolic plans and the evolving physical world, manifested as three recurring limitations: (i) existing systems often rely on pre-scanned semantic maps that become inconsistent after scene changes and disturbances; (ii) they select navigation endpoints without considering downstream manipulation feasibility, causing the "arrived but inoperable" problem; and (iii) they handle anomalies through undifferentiated global replanning, which often fails to contain local errors. To address this execution inconsistency, we present ANCHOR, a physically grounded closed-loop framework that aligns symbolic reasoning with verifiable physical state during execution. ANCHOR integrates three mechanisms: (i) physically anchored task planning, which binds symbolic predicates to observable geometric anchors and re-validates them after each action; (ii) operability-aware base alignment, which ensures that navigation endpoints satisfy kinematic reachability and local collision feasibility; and (iii) minimum-responsible-layer hierarchical recovery, which localizes failures across perception, base-arm coordination, and execution layers to prevent cascading retries. Across 60 real-robot trials in previously unseen environments, ANCHOR improves task success from 53.3% to 71.7% and achieves a 71.4% recovery rate under perturbations, demonstrating that explicit physical grounding and structured failure containment are critical for robust mobile manipulation. Our project page is available at https://anchor9178.github.io/ANCHOR/ .

</details>


### [61] [ProDrive: Proactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Ego-Environment Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25329)
*Chuyao Fu,Shengzhe Gan,Zhuoli Ouyang,Yuhan Rui,Xiaowei Chi,Sirui Han,Jiankun Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProDrive提出了一种基于世界模型的主动规划框架，通过端到端联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器与BEV世界模型，实现自车-环境的协同演化，在NAVSIM v1数据集上超越了当前仅依赖观测的反应式规划方法，显著提升了安全性与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器仅依赖当前观测生成轨迹，无法预判未来场景演化，导致近视决策和安全隐患。

Method: 端到端联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器与BEV世界模型：规划器生成多样化候选轨迹及规划感知的自车token，世界模型基于此预测未来场景演化；通过将规划器特征注入世界模型并并行评估所有候选轨迹，保持端到端梯度流动，使未来结果评估直接反馈优化规划。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1数据集上，ProDrive在安全性和规划效率方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 双向耦合的自车-环境协同设计实现了超越当前观测驱动的主动规划，消融实验验证了该耦合设计的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners typically generate trajectories from current observations alone. However, real-world driving is highly dynamic, and such reactive planning cannot anticipate future scene evolution, often leading to myopic decisions and safety-critical failures. We propose ProDrive, a world-model-based proactive planning framework that enables ego-environment co-evolution for autonomous driving. ProDrive jointly trains a query-centric trajectory planner and a bird's-eye-view (BEV) world model end-to-end: the planner generates diverse candidate trajectories and planning-aware ego tokens, while the world model predicts future scene evolution conditioned on them. By injecting planner features into the world model and evaluating all candidates in parallel, ProDrive preserves end-to-end gradient flow and allows future outcome assessment to directly shape planning. This bidirectional coupling enables proactive planning beyond current-observation-driven decision-making. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 show that ProDrive outperforms strong baselines in both safety and planning efficiency, while ablations validate the effectiveness of the proposed ego-environment coupling design.

</details>


### [62] [Robust Graph Matching through Semantic Relationship Generation for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25404)
*David Perez-Saura,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Holger Voos,Pascual Campoy,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义增强图匹配方法，通过建模物体与结构元素（房间、墙面）之间的关系，在对称/重复布局环境中减少模糊性，提升图匹配的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化室内环境中，基于场景图的定位方法在重复或对称布局下，仅依靠结构线索难以消除歧义，现有方法匹配效率低且易失败。

Method: 从RGB-D数据中检测物体并集成到图中，显式建模物体与结构元素（房间、墙面）之间的关系，利用语义关系过滤候选对应关系，再进行几何验证。

Result: 在合成和模拟环境中，语义关系显著减少了候选匹配数量，提升了计算效率，在对称场景中实现了更快的收敛，而纯几何方法在此类场景中失败。

Conclusion: 语义增强的图匹配方法能有效解决对称/重复布局下的室内定位歧义问题，提升匹配效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph-based representations such as Scene Graphs enable localization in structured indoor environments by matching a locally observed graph, constructed from sensor data, to a prior map. This process is particularly challenging in environments with repetitive or symmetric layouts, where structural cues alone are often insufficient to resolve ambiguities. We propose a semantic-enhanced graph matching approach that explicitly models relations between detected objects and structural elements, such as rooms and wall planes. Objects are detected from RGB-D data and integrated into the graph, and their relations to structural elements are exploited to filter candidate correspondences prior to geometric verification, significantly reducing ambiguity and search complexity. The proposed method is integrated within the iS-Graphs framework and evaluated in synthetic and simulated environments. Results show that semantic relations significantly reduce the number of candidate matches, improve computational efficiency, and enable faster convergence, particularly in symmetric scenarios where purely geometric approaches fail.

</details>


### [63] [GS-Playground: A High-Throughput Photorealistic Simulator for Vision-Informed Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25459)
*Yufei Jia,Heng Zhang,Ziheng Zhang,Junzhe Wu,Mingrui Yu,Zifan Wang,Dixuan Jiang,Zheng Li,Chenyu Cao,Zhuoyuan Yu,Xun Yang,Haizhou Ge,Yuchi Zhang,Jiayuan Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Tianle Liu,Shenyu Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Bin Xie,Xuran Yao,Xiwa Deng,Guangyu Wang,Jinzhi Zhang,Lei Hao,Zhixing Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Anqi Wang,Hongyun Tian,Yiyi Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yizhou Jiang,Hanyang Shao,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Bokui Chen,Wei Sui,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Playground是一个多模态仿真框架，通过集成批量3D高斯泼溅渲染与高性能并行物理引擎，实现10^4 FPS的视觉渲染吞吐量，并引入自动化Real2Sim工作流，有效加速具身AI的端到端感知学习，弥合了视觉与物理之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模并行模拟器在基于本体感觉的 locomotion 任务中取得了突破，但在视觉感知任务中受限于两大瓶颈：(1) 大规模逼真渲染的计算开销过高；(2) 仿真就绪3D资产的创建依赖繁琐的人工建模，且仿真到现实的物理差距阻碍了接触式操作策略的迁移。

Method: 提出GS-Playground框架：(1) 设计高性能并行物理引擎，与批量3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)渲染管线集成，确保高保真同步；(2) 引入自动化Real2Sim工作流，重建逼真、物理一致且内存高效的仿真环境。

Result: 系统在640×480分辨率下达到10^4 FPS的突破性吞吐量，显著降低了大规模视觉强化学习的准入门槛。

Conclusion: 在移动、导航和操作任务的广泛实验表明，GS-Playground有效弥合了多种具身任务中的视觉和物理差距。

Abstract: Embodied AI research is undergoing a shift toward vision-centric perceptual paradigms. While massively parallel simulators have catalyzed breakthroughs in proprioception-based locomotion, their potential remains largely untapped for vision-informed tasks due to the prohibitive computational overhead of large-scale photorealistic rendering. Furthermore, the creation of simulation-ready 3D assets heavily relies on labor-intensive manual modeling, while the significant sim-to-real physical gap hinders the transfer of contact-rich manipulation policies. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GS-Playground, a multi-modal simulation framework designed to accelerate end-to-end perceptual learning. We develop a novel high-performance parallel physics engine, specifically designed to integrate with a batch 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering pipeline to ensure high-fidelity synchronization. Our system achieves a breakthrough throughput of 10^4 FPS at 640x480 resolution, significantly lowering the barrier for large-scale visual RL. Additionally, we introduce an automated Real2Sim workflow that reconstructs photorealistic, physically consistent, and memory-efficient environments, streamlining the generation of complex simulation-ready scenes. Extensive experiments on locomotion, navigation, and manipulation demonstrate that GS-Playground effectively bridges the perceptual and physical gaps across diverse embodied tasks. Project homepage: https://gsplayground.github.io.

</details>


### [64] [Egocentric Tactile and Proximity Sensors as Observation Priors for Humanoid Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25554)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Niraj Pudasaini,William Xie,Naren Sivagnanadasan,Nikolaus Correll,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于强化学习的全身避障框架，用于人形机器人H1-2，并系统分析了传感器属性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）对避障行为的影响。以躲避球为基准任务，发现原始近距离测量可替代显式目标定位（需足够传感距离），且稀疏非定向信号在样本效率上优于密集定向信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉和接近传感器在机器人身上具有抗遮挡优势，但如何设计传感器的覆盖范围、类型和距离以实现有效的避障行为尚不明确。

Method: 使用强化学习框架，在H1-2人形机器人上进行全身避障训练，以躲避球为基准任务，对分布于机器人上半身的传感器属性进行消融实验。

Result: 原始近距离测量在足够传感范围内可替代显式目标定位；稀疏非定向接近信号在样本效率上优于密集定向信号。

Conclusion: 传感器属性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）对学习到的避障行为有显著影响，为机器人本体传感器布局设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Collision-free motion is often aided by tactile and proximity sensors distributed on the body of the robot due to their resistance to occlusion as opposed to external cameras. However, how to shape the sensor's properties, such as sensing coverage; type; and range, to enable avoidant behavior remains unclear. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning framework for whole-body collision avoidance on a humanoid H1-2 robot and use it to characterize how sensor properties shape learned avoidance behavior. Using dodgeball as a benchmark task, we ablate the properties of sensors distributed across the upper body of the robot and find that raw proximity measurements can substitute for explicit object localization provided the sensing range is sufficient and that sparse non-directional proximity signals outpace dense directional alternatives in sample efficiency.

</details>


### [65] [Improving Sensing Coverage and Compliance of 3D-Printed Artificial Skins Through Multi-Modal Sensing and Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25563)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Caleb Escobedo,Sayak Ray,Alexander Dickhans,Anna Soukhovei,Nickolaus Jackoski,Lyle Antieau,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种3D打印混合ToF与自电容传感的人造皮肤，实现多模态感知、柔软覆盖及简化电气接口，并在机器人手臂上部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印人造皮肤局限于单模态传感和刚性材料，实用性不足，需要多模态集成与柔软覆盖以提升实际可用性。

Method: 将飞行时间（ToF）与自电容（SC）传感模式结合，设计柔软覆盖层用于冲击吸收与压力感知，并优化导电痕迹与外部电子器件的电气接口。

Result: 在FR3机器人手臂上部署6个人造皮肤单元（40个传感元件），验证了接触检测、场景重建及压力相关触觉响应的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合ToF-SC传感皮肤实现了多模态集成与软体兼容性，为3D打印人造皮肤走向实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: 3D-printed artificial skins are a scalable approach to whole-body tactile and proximity coverage, but prior implementations have been limited to unimodal sensing and rigid materials. To improve the practical usability of 3D-printed artificial skins, we present a hybrid time-of-flight (ToF) and self-capacitance (SC) sensing skin that demonstrates multi-modal sensing integration, soft compliant coverings for impact absorption and pressure sensing, and a streamlined electrical interface between printed conductive traces and external electronics. We show that combining ToF and SC modalities enables contact detection, scene reconstruction, and pressure-correlated tactile responses with the compliant covering by deploying six artificial skin units with 40 sensing elements over an FR3 robot arm.

</details>


### [66] [SlicerRoboTMS: An Open-Source 3D Slicer Extension for Robot-Assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25661)
*Wenzhi Bai,Yituo Guo,Bhaskar Basu,Andrew Weightman,Zhenhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SlicerRoboTMS，一个基于3D Slicer的开源扩展，为机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）研究提供统一的交互基础设施，支持MRI导航和机器人系统集成，降低了该多学科领域的研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robo-TMS的开发需要医学影像、计算机视觉和机器人学等多学科专业知识，缺乏统一的交互平台，导致研发门槛高、可重复性差。

Method: 基于3D Slicer平台开发开源扩展，支持MRI神经导航，通过标准化通信协议和可配置系统描述与机器人系统对接，提供统一交互基础设施。

Result: 实现了与代表性Robo-TMS工作流的集成，降低了研究门槛，支持多样化硬件配置和快速原型开发。

Conclusion: SlicerRoboTMS作为开源工具，促进了Robo-TMS研究的可重复性和可扩展性，为多学科研究提供了便捷的基础设施。

Abstract: Robot-assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (Robo-TMS) is an image-guided robotic intervention that enhances the accuracy and reproducibility of conventional Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (TMS), a widely used non-invasive brain stimulation procedure in clinical treatment and neuroscience research. Despite its potential, the development of Robo-TMS remains challenging due to the need for multidisciplinary expertise spanning medical imaging, computer vision, and robotics. This paper presents SlicerRoboTMS, an open-source 3D Slicer extension that provides a unified interaction infrastructure for Robo-TMS research. By leveraging 3D Slicer's medical image computing and visualisation capabilities, the extension supports Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-based neuronavigation and interfaces with robotic systems through standardised communication protocols and configurable system descriptions. An example integration is presented to demonstrate how SlicerRoboTMS can be incorporated into a representative Robo-TMS workflow. Designed to support diverse hardware configurations and rapid prototyping, SlicerRoboTMS lowers the barrier to entry and facilitates reproducible and extensible research in Robo-TMS. The extension is available at https://github.com/OpenRoboTMS/SlicerRoboTMS.

</details>


### [67] [GEGLU-Transformer for IMU-to-EMG Estimation with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25670)
*Miroljub Mihailovic,Luca Tonin,Stefano Tortora,Emanuele Menegatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应IMU-to-EMG学习框架（GEGLU-Transformer），通过惯性测量单元信号重建肌肉激活包络，在跨受试者泛化和快速个性化方面表现出色，为可穿戴机器人提供了一种可替代直接EMG传感的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号（EMG）在实验室外难以鲁棒部署，存在电极敏感性、信号非平稳性和强受试者依赖性等问题，因此需要一种可靠的神经肌肉激活估计方法，以实现可穿戴机器人的自适应和个性化控制。

Method: 提出GEGLU-Transformer框架，结合Transformer编码器与高斯误差门控线性单元，从可穿戴惯性测量单元（IMU）数据中连续重建肌肉激活包络；采用严格留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）协议进行多条件下肢生物力学数据集评估。

Result: 无受试者适应时r=0.706±0.139，R²=0.474±0.208；仅用0.5%适应数据后性能提升至r=0.761±0.030，R²=0.559±0.047，表现出快速适应和早期性能饱和特性。

Conclusion: 基于注意力机制的架构结合轻量级适应策略，可作为直接EMG传感的实用且可扩展的替代方案，适用于现实世界中的可穿戴机器人应用。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of neuromuscular activation is a key enabler for adaptive and personalized control in wearable robotics. However, surface electromyography (EMG) remains difficult to deploy robustly outside laboratory settings due to electrode sensitivity, signal non-stationarity, and strong subject dependence. In this work, we propose an adaptive IMU-to-EMG learning framework that reconstructs continuous muscle activation envelopes from wearable inertial measurements across heterogeneous movement conditions. The approach combines a Transformer encoder with Gaussian Error Gated Linear Units (GEGLU-Transformer) to enhance cross-subject generalization and enable rapid subject-specific personalization. Under a strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol on a multi-condition lower-limb biomechanics dataset, the proposed architecture achieves r = 0.706 +/- 0.139 and R^2 = 0.474 +/- 0.208 without subject-specific adaptation. With only 0.5% adaptation data, performance increases to r = 0.761 +/- 0.030 and R^2 = 0.559 +/- 0.047, demonstrating rapid adaptation and early performance saturation. These results support attention-based architectures combined with lightweight adaptation as a practical and scalable alternative to direct EMG sensing for real-world wearable robotic applications.

</details>


### [68] [Learning-Based Dynamics Modeling and Robust Control for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25691)
*Ziqing Zou,Ke Qiu,Fei Wang,Haojian Lu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可微学习框架，集成高保真动力学建模与鲁棒神经控制，用于解决 tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) 的摩擦滞后和传动柔顺等复杂非线性问题。该方法采用 GRU 基础的双向多通道残差预测模型抑制长程自回归误差累积，并通过梯度桥接实现端到端神经控制策略优化。在三段 TDCR 上验证表明，该方法在轨迹跟踪精度和抗未知负载鲁棒性上优于 Jacobian 方法，且消除了自激振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDCRs 因摩擦滞后和传动柔顺等复杂非线性，导致建模与控制极具挑战性，现有方法难以兼顾高保真动力学建模与鲁棒控制。

Method: 提出可微学习框架：1) 构建 GRU 基础的双向多通道残差预测动力学模型，抑制长程自回归误差累积；2) 将动力学模型作为梯度桥接，通过反向传播端到端优化神经控制策略，隐式内化非线性补偿。

Result: 在三段 TDCR 上实验验证，该方法实现了精确轨迹跟踪，对未知负载具有优越鲁棒性，且消除了 Jacobian 方法中的自激振荡问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的可微学习框架有效解决了 TDCRs 的建模与控制难题，在跟踪精度和鲁棒性上均优于传统 Jacobian 方法。

Abstract: Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant modeling and control challenges due to complex nonlinearities, such as frictional hysteresis and transmission compliance. This paper proposes a differentiable learning framework that integrates high-fidelity dynamics modeling with robust neural control. We develop a GRU-based dynamics model featuring bidirectional multi-channel connectivity and residual prediction to effectively suppress compounding errors during long-horizon auto-regressive prediction. By treating this model as a gradient bridge, an end-to-end neural control policy is optimized through backpropagation, allowing it to implicitly internalize compensation for intricate nonlinearities. Experimental validation on a physical three-section TDCR demonstrates that our framework achieves accurate tracking and superior robustness against unseen payloads, outperforming Jacobian-based methods by eliminating self-excited oscillations.

</details>


### [69] [Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25766)
*Giuseppe Silano,Quentin Sablé,Marco Tognon,Luigi Iannelli,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管式非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于有界参数不确定条件下的协作空中链系统。针对由刚性连杆连接的双飞行器平面链，采用输入速率驱动来约束推力和力矩的转换速率与幅值。通过沿预测时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度并在线计算约束收紧余量，实现对连杆质量、长度和惯量不确定性的鲁棒性。通过平滑余弦嵌入实现链间距约束的鲁棒化，并约束推力幅值边界。MATLAB实现及边界逼近机动与蒙特卡洛不确定性采样评估显示，该方法在保持与标称NMPC相当跟踪性能的同时，改善了不确定性下的约束余量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMPC方法在处理协作空中链系统的参数不确定性（如连杆质量、长度、惯量）时缺乏鲁棒性保证，需要一种能够在线计算约束收紧余量的方法以应对有界参数不确定性。

Method: 基于灵敏度的管式NMPC框架：沿预测时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度，利用灵敏度信息在线计算约束收紧余量；通过平滑余弦嵌入实现链间距约束的鲁棒化；采用输入速率驱动模型约束推力和力矩。

Result: 在边界逼近机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样测试中，该方法在保持与标称NMPC相当跟踪性能的同时，显著改善了不确定性下的约束满足情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的灵敏度管式NMPC能够有效处理有界参数不确定性，在保证约束鲁棒性的同时维持良好的跟踪性能，适用于协作空中链系统的安全控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.

</details>


### [70] [KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25788)
*Yixuan Huang,Bowen Li,Vaibhav Saxena,Yichao Liang,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Liang Ji,Lihan Zha,Jimmy Wu,Nishanth Kumar,Sebastian Scherer,Danfei Xu,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KinDER是一个针对机器人在运动学和动力学 embodied reasoning 的基准，包含25个程序化生成的环境、Gymnasium兼容库、标准化评估套件及13个基线方法，旨在隔离核心物理推理挑战。实验表明现有方法难以解决多数环境，揭示了当前物理推理的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习和规划方法在物理推理方面存在不足，缺乏一个系统性的基准来评估机器人对运动学约束和动力学约束的推理能力。作者希望构建一个可隔离核心物理推理挑战的标准化评估平台。

Method: 设计了25个程序化生成的环境，涵盖5个核心物理推理挑战（基本空间关系、非抓取式多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束和动态约束）；提供了Gymnasium兼容的Python库、参数化技能和演示数据；实现了13个基线方法（涵盖任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习和基础模型方法）；包含真实到仿真到真实（real-to-sim-to-real）实验。

Result: 现有方法在大多数环境下表现不佳，表明当前物理推理方法存在显著差距。real-to-sim-to-real实验验证了仿真与现实物理交互之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: KinDER提供了一个系统化的基准，能够支持跨不同范式（TAMP、IL、RL、基础模型）的物理推理能力比较，有助于推动机器人物理推理的研究。代码已开源。

Abstract: Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [The Effects of Population Size on the Performance of BEAGLE GPU-Based Genetic Programming Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24968)
*Nathan Haut,Ilya Basin,Ruchika Gupta,Marzieh Kianinejad,Zachary Perrico,Elijah Smith,Wolfgang Banzhaf*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Beagle框架利用GPU遗传编程实现了CPU系统无法在合理时间内完成的大规模种群动态，并研究了不同种群大小对符号回归问题训练效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GPU加速的遗传编程能否通过大规模种群实现以往CPU系统无法在合理时间内完成的种群动态，并研究不同种群规模策略对符号回归问题训练成功率的影响。

Method: 使用Beagle框架在GPU上执行遗传编程，测试恒定种群大小（从1000到1000万个体）以及阶梯式种群大小（从大种群逐步降至小种群）两种策略。

Result: 恒定种群下，不同问题对搜索策略偏好不同：部分问题受益于窄而深的搜索（小至1000个体），部分受益于宽而浅的搜索（大至1000万个体）。阶梯式种群能够平衡搜索的广度和深度。

Conclusion: GPU遗传编程能够实现大规模种群动态，且种群策略的选择对问题求解效果有显著影响，阶梯式种群是兼顾广度与深度的有效策略。

Abstract: The Beagle framework, through GPU-based Genetic Programming, enables population dynamics previously unattainable (within practical time frames) by CPU-constrained Genetic Programming systems. This work explores how GPU-enabled population sizes impact the success of training for symbolic regression problems. Specifically, when using constant population sizes, we see benefits of using very narrow and deep searches (as narrow as 1000 individuals) for some problems, while other problems benefit from very broad and shallow searches (as broad as 10 million individuals). We also explore stepped population sizes that start with large populations and drop to small populations to balance the breadth and depth of search.

</details>


### [72] [Benchmarking Stopping Criteria for Evolutionary Multi-objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25458)
*Kenji Kitamura,Ryoji Tanabe*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文针对进化多目标优化（EMO）中停止准则研究停滞的问题，提出了三项贡献：停止准则的性能度量方法、基于文件的基准测试方法以及高效的数据表示方法，并通过五个代表性停止准则验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停止准则在进化算法的实际应用中至关重要，可以避免在停滞种群上浪费函数评估次数。然而，在进化多目标优化（EMO）领域，停止准则的研究一直较少，近年来几乎没有新的停止准则被提出。造成这一现象的主要原因之一是缺乏有效的基准测试方法。

Method: (i) 提出一种将停止准则性能表示为单一标量值的度量方法，便于比较；(ii) 提出基于文件的基准测试方法，简化流程并提升可重复性；(iii) 提出一种有效存储种群状态的数据表示方法，解决文件体积过大问题。通过五个代表性停止准则进行实验验证。

Result: 通过五项代表性停止准则的基准测试，验证了所提出的性能度量、文件基准测试方法和数据表示方法三者组合的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三项贡献有效解决了EMO停止准则缺乏基准测试方法的问题，为未来停止准则的开发与公平比较提供了标准化工具，有望推动该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Stopping criteria automatically determine when to stop an evolutionary algorithm, so as not to waste function evaluations on a stagnant population. Although stopping criteria play an important role in real-world applications, they have attracted little attention in the evolutionary multi-objective optimization (EMO) community. In fact, new stopping criteria for EMO have been rarely developed in recent years. One reason for the stagnation in developing stopping criteria for EMO is a lack of effective benchmarking methodologies. To address this issue, this paper proposes (i) a performance measure of stopping criteria for EMO and (ii) a file-based benchmarking approach. This paper also proposes (iii) a data representation method that effectively stores population states in text files. (i) The proposed measure represents the performance of stopping criteria as a single scalar value, making comparison easy. (ii) The proposed file-based approach not only simplifies the benchmarking process but also facilitates reproducibility. (iii) The proposed data representation method addresses the issue of file size in (ii). We demonstrate the effectiveness of our three contributions (i)--(iii) by benchmarking five representative stopping criteria for EMO.

</details>

### [73] [Vega-Video: Integrating Video into the Grammar of Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24958)
*Dominik Winecki,Arnab Nandi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出将视频数据集成到Vega声明式语法中的三类可视化抽象（同步、标注、转换），通过分裂信号架构和编译时优化，实现了高性能的视频与常规数据混合可视化，关键更新延迟低于200ms，响应速度提升达4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频数据在交互式数据探索中日益重要，但现有可视化系统难以有效集成视频，因其独特的范式（时间序列播放）和性能挑战（更新延迟）与现有声明式语法的瞬时数据流模型不兼容。

Method: 1) 识别三类视频数据可视化抽象（同步、标注、转换）并将其融入Vega语法；2) 提出分裂信号架构，将视频播放器状态与常规数据流分离，以掩盖视频更新延迟；3) 在编译时检测连续擦洗操作，应用编码感知优化使响应速度提升4倍；4) 改造VOD协议实现实时视频转换，支持亚200ms更新。

Result: 实现了混合模态数据（视频+常规数据）的无缝可视化集成，关键更新延迟低于200ms，连续交互场景下响应速度提升达4倍，即使对长达数小时的视频也能保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 通过将视频数据纳入声明式可视化框架并解决其性能瓶颈，使得视频可以像常规数据一样被声明式地探索和呈现，拓展了可视化系统的表达能力与应用场景。

Abstract: Video data is increasingly used alongside conventional data for interactive data exploration, necessitating interfaces for exploring and presenting mixed-modality data. However, integrating video into visualizations remains difficult due to its distinct paradigms and inherent performance challenges. We identify three classes of video data visualization - synchronization, annotation, and transformation - and integrate them into the Vega declarative grammar. We show that these abstractions enable high-performance implementation. To reconcile Vega's instantaneous dataflow with video player state, we introduce a split-signal architecture that preserves declarative semantics while masking video update delays. We detect continuous scrubbing interactions at compile time to apply encoding-aware optimizations that improve responsiveness by up to 4x. We also repurpose VOD protocols to transform videos in real time, delivering sub-200ms updates even on multi-hour-long compilations. These contributions enable seamless integration of conventional and video data visualization.

</details>


### [74] [What If We Work Together? Fostering Reflections on Designer Inclusion in Open Source Software Through Speculative Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24981)
*Rozhan Hozhabri Nezhad,Jin L. C. Guo,Jinghui Cheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过创建两个思辨设计社会（集体主义的Husia和个人主义的Reetar），探究如何利用思辨设计激发开源社区对设计师包容性环境的反思，从而解决开源软件中缺乏设计专业知识和以开发者为中心的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件通常优先考虑技术功能而非可用性和用户体验设计，导致难以被非技术用户广泛采用。根本原因在于开源社区缺乏设计专业知识以及以开发者为中心的思维模式。

Method: 首先分析在线论坛以了解设计师参与开源的动机和挑战；基于这些见解创建两个思辨社会（集体主义的Husia和个人主义的Reetar）；通过12名开源从业者（7名设计师、5名开发者）的用户研究评估效果。

Result: 思辨社会引发了参与者对开源价值观、挑战根源和拟议行动的丰富而批判性的反思。

Conclusion: 思辨设计可在开源这一实际的社会技术背景下用于激发批判性反思、提高意识，并产生促进公平、可持续和包容性开源环境的建议。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) often prioritizes technical functionality over usability and UX design. This imbalance limits OSS adoption among broader, non-technical users. Key underlying factors contributing to this issue are the shortage of design expertise in OSS and a dominant developer-centric mindset. To address these persistent issues, we explore the potential of speculative design as a catalyst for transforming the OSS community's mindset towards a more designer-inclusive environment. Our design was informed by an analysis of online forums, which revealed designers' motivations and challenges when contributing to OSS. Guided by these insights, we created two speculative societies, Husia (collectivist) and Reetar (individualist), in which designers are valued for different reasons and their work incorporated in different ways. Through a user study with 12 OSS practitioners (seven designers and five developers), we found that our speculative societies provoked participants' rich and critical reflections on OSS values, the root causes of challenges, and proposed actions. Our work provides insights into how speculative design can be used in the practical, sociotechnical context of OSS to stimulate critical reflection, improve awareness, and yield recommendations for fostering an equitable, sustainable, and inclusive OSS environment.

</details>


### [75] ["We Wanted to Do Better Than the Law": Exploring UI/UX Designers' Privacy Advocacy in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24982)
*Keyu Yao,Jinghui Cheng,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对12位隐私倡导型UI/UX设计师的半结构化访谈，本研究揭示了设计师在团队协作环境中践行隐私设计时所面临的挑战、适应策略及其影响因素，并提出以用户为中心支持隐私意识设计的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究主要关注开发者在技术产品隐私增强中的角色，而较少探讨UI/UX设计师如何考虑和处理隐私问题，因此本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对12位隐私倡导型UI/UX设计师进行半结构化访谈，探讨其对隐私实现的认知、影响因素、挑战及适应方法，特别关注团队协商决策情境下的挑战与适应。

Result: 揭示了个人和情境因素如何塑造设计师对隐私的价值观、设计师在优先考虑隐私时面临的协作性挑战的本质，以及他们如何在商业目标、团队动态和技术开发之间权衡妥协。

Conclusion: 提出倡导以用户为中心的方法支持隐私意识设计、在组织层面进行变革的建议，以及通过设计师为中心的工具和社区建设弥合知识差距。

Abstract: Designers hold primary responsibility for shaping the user interface (UI) and user experience (UX) of a product. This role goes beyond aesthetics and usability, extending to the privacy outcomes of user experience, which often emerge through collaboration with other stakeholders such as developers, product managers, and marketing teams. Previous studies on enhancing privacy for technological products primarily focused on the roles of developers -- understanding their needs and challenges -- but limited effort is devoted to examining how UI/UX designers consider and approach privacy in their work. Through 12 semi-structured interviews with privacy-advocating UI/UX designers, we explore the perceptions, influencing factors, challenges, and adaptive methods they use regarding privacy implementation. We pay special attention to how these challenges and adaptations play out in team-based settings where decisions are negotiated together. Our study reveals how personal and contextual factors shape designers' value of privacy, the collaborative nature of the challenges designers face when trying to prioritize privacy, and how they navigate tensions between business goals, team dynamics, and technical development. Based on our findings, we discuss implications for advocating a user-centered approach for supporting privacy-aware design, suggestions for organizational-level changes and bridging knowledge gaps through designer-centric tools and community building.

</details>


### [76] [People, IT, and Structuration (PIS): An Integrative Theoretical Framework for Management Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25118)
*Wei Huang,Xiaofang Cai,Qiaozhen Guo,Xiaosong Wu,Xin Tang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出人-IT-结构化(PIS)框架，整合MIS领域碎片化的理论流，将人、信息技术和结构视为相互构成的要素，通过结构化过程解释三者的协同演化，并扩展至人工智能、算法管理等当代现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIS领域长期缺乏统一理论框架来阐明人、信息技术与组织结构之间复杂的相互构成关系，现有理论流（如社会技术系统、技术接受模型、适应性结构化理论、社会物质性等）虽各有洞见但碎片化，亟待整合性理论视角。

Method: 基于吉登斯结构化理论，提出PIS框架，将人(P)、信息技术(I)和结构(S)概念化为结构化过程中相互构成的要素；通过梳理MIS理论史论证框架如何消解技术决定论与社会决定论、方差与过程、微观与宏观等张力；发展形式化命题阐明三者的协同演化机制。

Result: PIS框架能够统一解释MIS领域的多元理论传统，有效消解长期存在的二元论张力，并为人工智能、算法管理、人机协作等新兴现象提供理论分析工具。

Conclusion: PIS框架既是理解MIS学科理论演化的回顾性透镜，也是指导AI时代研究的前瞻性工具，为推进MIS理论整合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Management Information Systems (MIS) discipline has long grappled with how to theorize the complex, mutually constitutive relationships among people, information technology, and organizational structures. Decades of research have produced influential but fragmented theoretical streams from socio-technical systems theory to technology acceptance models, from adaptive structuration theory to sociomateriality, and each illuminating important facets while leaving integrative questions unresolved. This paper proposes the People - IT - Structuration (PIS) framework as a unifying theoretical lens that synthesizes these streams. Drawing on Giddens' structuration theory, we conceptualize People (P), Information Technology (I), and Structure (S) not as independent variables but as mutually constitutive elements engaged in ongoing structuration processes. We trace the intellectual history of MIS theorizing to demonstrate how PIS resolves persistent tensions in the field,e.g. between technological and social determinism, between variance and process approaches, and between micro-level interaction and macro-level institutional dynamics. We develop a set of formal propositions articulating the mechanisms through which P, I, and S co-evolve, and extend the framework to address contemporary phenomena including artificial intelligence, algorithmic management, and human-AI collaboration. The PIS framework offers both a retrospective lens for understanding the discipline's theoretical evolution and a prospective tool for guiding research in the AI era.

</details>


### [77] [Value-Sensitive AI for Prayer: Balancing the Agencies Between Human and AI Agents in Spiritual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25230)
*Soonho Kwon,Dong Whi Yoo,Shaowen Bardzell,Younah Kang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过日记研究和思辨设计工作坊，探讨AI系统如何影响祷告体验，发现真实性（与神圣的真实连接）是核心价值，而AI过度介入会削弱这种真实性，因此提出AI系统设计应保持解释开放性、利用AI的不可解释性作为个人意义建构的资源，或承认非使用AI也是合理的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统的介入如何影响高度价值负载的祷告体验，识别其中关键价值，并为这类敏感场景的AI设计提供指导原则。

Method: 通过日记研究识别祷告实践中的关键价值和行为，设计四种概念性价值敏感AI系统，利用思辨设计工作簿作为触发物，与参与者共同反思AI对祷告体验的影响。

Result: 发现"真实性"（与神圣连接的真实感）是核心价值；AI在场常被感知为削弱这种真实性，尤其是当AI在引导祷告实践方面承担过多能动性时。

Conclusion: 为深度价值负载体验设计AI系统时，应保留用户塑造自身体验的能动性，通过保持解释开放性、利用AI的不可解释性作为个人意义建构的资源、以及将非使用AI视为合法设计选择来实现。

Abstract: We present four conceptual value-sensitive AI systems to examine how the presence of AI could influence praying experiences. Drawing on key values and practices associated with praying identified through a diary study, we designed AI systems intended to "assist" prayer practices. These designs were presented to participants through speculative design workbooks, serving as provocations to co-reflect on how the intervention of AI systems might shape their praying experiences. Our findings suggest that a sense of authenticity (or feeling a genuine connection to the divine) is a crucial value, while the presence of AI was often perceived as diminishing this authenticity, particularly when AI assumed too much agency in guiding praying practices. Based on our findings, we argue that AI system designs for deeply value-laden experiences should preserve users' agency in shaping their own experiences by maintaining interpretive openness, perhaps by leveraging AI's inexplicability as a resource for personal meaning-making or by recognizing non-use of AI as a legitimate design choice.

</details>


### [78] [Visual Boosting Techniques for Spatiotemporal Dense Pixel Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25298)
*Julius Rauscher,Frederik L. Dennig,Udo Schlegel,Daniel A. Keim,Tobias Schreck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对时空数据密集像素可视化中因空间线性化产生的视觉伪影问题，提出了一种基于度量驱动的视觉分析方法，通过邻域保持度量捕获伪影，并利用字形、光晕、影线等视觉增强技术进行渲染，帮助分析师区分真实空间模式与线性化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空数据分析中，密集像素可视化虽能紧凑呈现时空动态，但将二维地理空间线性化为一维排序时不可避免地引入结构扭曲，产生视觉伪影，干扰分析师对真实空间模式的判断。

Method: 提出度量驱动的视觉分析方法：利用邻域保持度量量化一维排序中的结构扭曲；采用视觉增强技术（字形、光晕、影线）对伪影区域进行可视化渲染；支持交互式探索与分析。

Result: 以德国各区COVID-19发病率数据为用例，验证了该方法能使分析师通过交互式度量驱动增强，可靠地区分真实空间模式与线性化伪影。

Conclusion: 度量驱动的视觉增强方法有效解决了时空密集像素可视化中的线性化伪影问题，提升了分析可靠性。

Abstract: The analysis of spatiotemporal data is essential in domains such as epidemiology and environmental monitoring, where understanding the interplay between spatially distributed phenomena and their temporal evolution is critical. Dense pixel visualizations offer a compact, effective overview of spatiotemporal dynamics. However, the necessary linearization of 2D geographic space into a 1D ordering inevitably introduces structural distortions that manifest as visual artifacts. We propose a measure-driven visual analytics approach that captures visual artifacts through neighborhood preservation measures for 1D orderings and renders them using visual boosting techniques such as glyphs, halos, and hatching. We demonstrate our approach through a usage scenario analyzing COVID-19 incidence data across German districts, showing that interactive, measure-driven boosting enables analysts to reliably distinguish genuine spatial patterns from linearization artifacts.

</details>


### [79] [Author response to commentaries on H is for Human and How (Not) to Evaluate Qualitative Research in HCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25312)
*Andy Crabtree*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文是作者对HCI领域质性研究评价标准的回应与反思，回应了多位学者对原文章的评论，强调以“人”为中心而非机械套用评价标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应学术界对原文章提出的质性研究评价标准的批评与讨论，进一步澄清和完善观点。

Method: 通过对多位评论者（Bardzell, Blackwell, Dourish, Nardi, Pirolli, Rode, Tolmie）的评论进行逐一回应和讨论。

Result: 作者坚持质性研究应关注“人”的本质，而非过度依赖形式化的评价框架。

Conclusion: HCI领域的质性研究评价应回归人文本质，避免僵化的标准化评估。

Abstract: This is the authors response to commentaries on the original article H is for Human and How (Not) to Evaluate Qualitative Research in HCI, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2475743 Commentaries were provided by: Jeffrey Bardzell, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2612474 Alan Blackwell, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2591878 Paul Dourish, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2594529 Bonnie Nardi, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2596752 Peter Pirolli, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2596745 Jennifer Rode, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2598800 Peter Tolmie, https://doi.org/10.1080/07370024.2025.2591872 Please feel free to copy, redistribute, adapt, and build on any part of this article in accordance with the CC BY 4.0 license: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/

</details>


### [80] [Co-Writing with AI: An Empirical Study of Diverse Academic Writing Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25389)
*Silvia Bodei,Duncan P. Brumby,Katie Fisher,Jon Mella*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对107名英国大学生进行问卷调查和对12名研究生进行访谈，研究发现学生在学术写作中利用AI的方式呈现选择性、异质性和价值导向性，形成了三种典型配置：早期阶段（学习导向）、后期阶段（质量导向）和外围阶段（效率导向）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI工具在学术实践中日益普及，但关于大学生如何在写作过程中整合这些工具的了解很少，因此本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用两阶段混合方法：研究1对107名英国大学生进行问卷调查，绘制AI在五个写作阶段（构思、资料查找、规划、起草、审阅）的任务特定和共现使用模式；研究2通过对12名研究生的访谈，探索这些模式如何在实践中组装。

Result: AI整合呈现选择性和异质性，形成了三种价值导向的配置：(1)早期阶段（学习导向）——支持探索和理解；(2)后期阶段（质量导向）——支持起草和精炼；(3)外围阶段（效率导向）——减少摩擦、维持动力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了工作流程层面的AI辅助学术写作图景，展示了学生如何在学习、质量、效率和作者身份等竞争性优先事项之间进行权衡，以及如何评估和对AI生成的内容承担责任。

Abstract: Despite AI tools becoming increasingly embedded in academic practice, little is known about how university students integrate them into their writing processes. We examine how students engage with AI across different writing tasks, and how this engagement is shaped by individual factors including AI literacy, writing confidence, trust, authorship concerns, and motivation. Study~1 surveys 107 UK university students to map task-specific and co-occurring patterns of AI use across five writing stages (ideation, sourcing, planning, drafting, and reviewing) and their associations with individual factors. Study~2 complements this by exploring how these patterns can be assembled in practice, through interviews with 12 postgraduates reflecting on their established use of AI in assessed writing. Together, the studies suggest that AI integration is selective and heterogeneous, forming three recurring and value-oriented configurations: (1) early-stage (learning-oriented), where tools support exploration and understanding; (2) late-stage (quality-oriented), where tools support drafting and refinement; and (3) peripheral (productivity-oriented), where tools are used to reduce friction and sustain momentum across the process. We offer a workflow-level account of AI-supported academic writing, showing how students navigate competing priorities of learning, quality, productivity, and authorship, and how they evaluate and take responsibility for AI-generated outputs.

</details>


### [81] [Rewiring Perceived Doability in VR: Hand Redirection as a Subtle Cross-Sensory Support for Sustained Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25443)
*Isidro Butaslac,Yota Nagaya,Almira Princess Redoble,Jordan Aiko Deja,Nicko Reginio Caluya,Maheshya Weerasinghe,Taishi Sawabe,Hirokazu Kato,Eric Cesar Vidal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用微幅虚拟手部重定向（HR）作为跨感官支持手段，通过创造重复的微小成功体验来提升个体对动作的"可完成性"感知，从而促进轻度运动的持续练习；同时探讨了这种支持在自主性和真实性方面的伦理张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们难以坚持轻度运动和拉伸锻炼，主要原因并非客观运动能力不足，而是当下感觉"做不做得到"的认知评估影响了持续意愿。作者希望探索一种无需外部压力或密集指导即可维持练习持续性的方法。

Method: 采用立场论文形式，提出将保守型VR手部重定向（HR）技术重新定义为跨感官支持机制；通过让用户以相似的身体动作更早触达虚拟目标，创造重复的微小成功体验，从而提升"可完成性"感知。同时提出两个研究问题，并搭建了初始的坐姿前伸VR原型。

Result: 提出了两个待验证的核心研究问题：HR如何改变可完成性感知以支持持续练习与积极行为改变；HR在何种条件下可作为可接受的支持，何时会因损害真实性、能动性、信任或导致依赖而适得其反。指出了关键设计张力。

Conclusion: 呼吁HCI社区就保留自主性的跨感官设计未来展开讨论；强调微幅重定向在感知阈值内的应用可以为持续性行为改变提供新路径，同时必须审慎处理其对用户自主性和真实体验的潜在冲击。

Abstract: In everyday life, physical effort is often minimized and convenience is prioritized, making it difficult for many people to sustain light exercise and stretching despite well-known long-term benefits. This challenge often arises not from objective movement limitations, but from whether an action feels doable in the moment and, therefore worth continuing. This position paper argues that subtle VR hand redirection (HR) can be reframed as a form of cross-sensory support for sustained practice by targeting perceived doability: a moment-to-moment cognitive appraisal that an action is within one's capability while requiring manageable effort. We propose that conservative HR, applied within known perceptual limits, can create repeated micro-success experiences (e.g., reaching a virtual goal earlier with similar physical movement). These micro-successes may increase continuation intention and early re-engagement without relying on overt pressure or intensive coaching. At the same time, such support raises questions about autonomy and authenticity. We therefore articulate two research questions: (RQ1) how HR shifts perceived doability to support sustained practice and positive behavior change; and (RQ2) when HR functions as acceptable support versus becoming counterproductive by undermining authenticity, agency, trust, or fostering dependence. We present an initial sit-and-reach VR prototype, outline a research plan, and identify key design tensions to spark community discussions on autonomy-preserving cross-sensory futures in HCI.

</details>


### [82] [Generative UI as an Accessibility Bridge: Lessons from C2C E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25455)
*Bektur Ryskeldiev*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于2022-2025年对盲人、低视力及老年用户在C2C电商平台上的六项研究，提出生成式UI可在使用点生成自适应界面，解决静态无障碍标准无法预见的屏障，并通过HTML重建、对话引导和音频辅助拍照三项干预措施论证了运行时生成技术的价值，进而探讨了其对HCI实践的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态无障碍标准和开发者合规模式在用户生成内容的平台（如C2C市场）上失效——照片模糊或偏离画面、描述缺失尺寸和状态信息、页面结构因列表而异——这些屏障是静态设计无法预见的，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 基于2022-2025年间对盲人、低视力及老年用户使用C2C市场的六项研究，提出并验证了三项生成式UI干预措施：面向屏幕阅读器的HTML重建、面向老年卖家的对话式引导、以及面向盲人卖家的音频引导拍照。

Result: 生成式UI能够在运行时生成自适应界面，填补静态标准留下的空白，有效解决UGC平台中多样化的无障碍问题。

Conclusion: 生成式UI超越了屏幕范畴，补充而非取代基于能力的设计；设计师的角色应从指定布局转变为指定策略。本文是CHI 2026研讨会立场论文的扩展arXiv版本。

Abstract: Web accessibility rests on static standards and developer compliance. That model frays in platforms where content is user-generated: photos arrive blurry or off-frame, descriptions skip size and condition, and page structure shifts from listing to listing. Drawing on six studies conducted between 2022 and 2025 with blind, low-vision, and older adult users of customer-to-customer (C2C) marketplaces, I argue that generative UI can produce adapted interfaces at the point of use, addressing barriers that static design cannot anticipate. Three interventions from this program -- HTML regeneration for screen readers, conversational guidance for older sellers, and audio-guided photo framing for blind sellers -- demonstrate how runtime generation can bridge gaps that standards leave open. I outline what these findings imply for HCI practice: generative UI extends beyond the screen, complements rather than replaces ability-based design, and shifts the designer's role from specifying layouts to specifying policies. This is an expanded arXiv version of a position paper accepted at the CHI 2026 workshop "What does Generative UI mean for HCI Practice?"

</details>


### [83] [Making the Invisible Visible: Toward Micro-Expression Visualization for Empathy in Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25505)
*Feiyang Yin,Isidro Butaslac,Patrick Gebhard,Monica Perusquia-Hernandez,Zhaofeng Niu,Taishi Sawabe,Hirokazu Kato*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将微表情转化为可感知情感线索的概念框架，旨在增强社交共情体验，并计划通过初步实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微表情研究主要聚焦于计算识别与检测，在人机交互与社交增强等人类中心场景中的应用十分有限，未能将微表情的潜在价值转化为实际的共情支持工具。

Method: 从社交增强视角出发，提出了一个微表情可视化的概念框架，将原本不可察觉的微表情转化为可感知的情感线索，并规划了一项受控条件下的初步实验来评估框架可行性。

Result: 文中尚未报告具体实验结果，仅描述了框架设计与初步研究规划。

Conclusion: 该框架有望通过可视化微表情增强人际共情体验，但仍需后续实验验证其实际效果与可行性。

Abstract: Micro-expressions are brief and subtle facial movements that convey nuanced affective information but often remain imperceptible during natural social interaction. Although prior research has primarily focused on computational recognition and spotting of micro-expressions, their application in human-centered contexts remains limited. From the perspective of social augmentation, this work proposes a conceptual framework for micro-expression visualization that transforms otherwise imperceptible micro-expressions into perceptible affective cues, with the aim of exploring their potential influence on empathic experience. Furthermore, we outline a planned pilot study to preliminarily assess the feasibility of this framework under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [84] [Emotive Architectures: The Role of LLMs in Adjusting Work Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25601)
*Lara Vartziotis,Tina Vartziotis,Frank Beutenmueller,Stella Salta,Konstantinos Moraitis,Miltiadis Katsaros,Sotirios Kotsopoulos*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在远程与混合办公背景下，物理环境、虚拟环境及其融合构成的混合空间条件，以及大语言模型（如ChatGPT）如何通过自然语言处理实时调节环境参数（光照、声学、界面配置），将静态空间转变为动态、情感响应的环境，并提出了一个融合技术创新与人本体验的共适应环境框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程与混合办公的普及使得物理与数字环境的融合成为新常态，但现有空间设计缺乏对环境动态适应性的系统研究，尤其是缺乏利用AI语言模型实现环境与人之间情感和行为层面双向交互的理论框架。

Method: 通过分析物理环境（物质与感官属性）、虚拟环境（沉浸式技术）及混合环境（数字与物理动态交互）三种空间条件，并引入大语言模型（LLMs）作为自然语言中介，实现实时环境参数调节（如照明、声学、界面配置），同时考量隐私、情感追踪、用户能动性等伦理维度，构建共适应环境的理论框架。

Result: 提出了一种以LLMs为桥梁的共适应环境框架，使静态工作空间转变为动态、情感接收型环境，并能通过自然语言信号调节空间属性以提升用户专注度、幸福感和参与度。

Conclusion: 融合AI语言模型与空间环境的共适应设计为混合办公空间提供了新范式，但在推广过程中需重视隐私保护、透明设计和用户自主权的伦理保障。

Abstract: In remote and hybrid work contexts, the integration of physical and digital environments is revolutionizing spatial experiences, collaboration, and interpersonal interactions. This study examines three fundamental spatial conditions: the physical environment, characterized by material and sensory attributes; the virtual environment, influenced by immersive technologies; and their fusion into hybrid environments where digital and physical components interact dynamically. The increasing number of AI tools in contemporary society, extensively utilized in both professional and personal spheres, has led to a varied landscape of developing technologies. For instance, ChatGPT has emerged as one of the most downloaded applications, a statistically substantiated fact that demonstrates the swift incorporation of language-based AI into daily life. It also underscores the function of large language models (LLMs) as meaningful bridges between concepts at reading emotional and behavioral signals via natural language. These models provide real-time modifications such as altering illumination, acoustics, or interface configurations, converting static settings into dynamic, emotionally receptive environments. We investigate the integration of language models into professional settings and their potential to enhance user experience by promoting focus, well-being, and engagement. The study investigates ethical concerns, including privacy, emotional tracking, and user agency, emphasizing the importance of inclusive and transparent design. This research formulates a framework for creating co-adaptive environments that merge technological innovation with human-centered experiences, offering a fresh viewpoint on responsive and supportive hybrid workspaces.

</details>


### [85] [Designing and Evaluating Next-Generation Learning Interfaces: Linking AI, HCI, and the Learning Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25721)
*Meng Xia,Yan Chen,Qiao Jin,Yang Shi,Paul Denny,Tiffany Barnes,Qingsong Wen,Vincent Aleven*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研讨会汇聚人工智能、人机交互和学习科学领域的研究者与实践者，聚焦人机协作学习界面的设计与评估，旨在通过跨学科对话识别共享挑战、设计原则和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对交互系统如何更好支持学习的系统性探讨，尤其在AI、HCI与学习科学的交叉领域存在空白。

Method: 通过组织跨学科研讨会，邀请AI、HCI和学习科学领域的研究者与实践者共同参与，聚焦人类-AI协作学习界面的设计与评估。

Result: 识别出共享挑战、设计原则和下一代学习技术的研究方向。

Conclusion: 跨学科对话有助于推动技术上稳健、以人为中心且具有教学基础的下一代学习技术的发展。

Abstract: This workshop addresses this gap by bringing together researchers and practitioners from AI, HCI, and the learning sciences to explore how interactive systems can better support learning. We focus on the design and evaluation of human-AI collaborative learning interfaces that are technically robust, human-centered, and pedagogically grounded. By fostering interdisciplinary dialogue, the workshop aims to identify shared challenges, design principles, and research directions for next-generation learning technologies.

</details>


### [86] [Lexical Anthropomorphization Influences on Moral Judgments of AI Bad Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25814)
*Jaime Banks,Nicholas David Bowman,Roman Saladino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 四项实验（N=1,020）表明，拟人化语言和设计线索对AI不良行为的道德判断影响甚微；道德违规类型（尤其是伤害和贬低类）才是最强预测因素，拟人化主要提升了AI不诚实能力的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 媒体和政策领域广泛使用拟人化语言描述AI，同时AI不良行为（如幻觉、不当言论）也备受关注。研究旨在检验拟人化语言是否以及如何影响人们对AI不道德行为的道德判断。

Method: 四项实验总计1,020名被试。研究1-3检验词汇拟人化启动与拟人化设计线索（图标、名称、自我指涉）在非道德错误情境下的交互作用；研究4将范围扩展至七种道德违规类型的真正不道德AI行为。

Result: 拟人化语言和设计线索对AI不良行为的道德判断影响甚微。当效应出现时，高拟人化启动仅提升了AI不诚实能力的感知。道德违规类型是最强预测因素，其中伤害和贬低类违规产生最广泛的负面性格评估。启动漂移、负晕效应和利己价值观是潜在重要预测因素。

Conclusion: 拟人化语言对AI道德判断的实际影响有限，道德违规类型本身才是决定性因素。研究挑战了"拟人化会显著改变AI道德评估"的普遍假设。

Abstract: Anthropomorphic language describing artificial intelligence (AI) is widespread in media, policy, and everyday discourse; so too are discussions of AI bad behavior, from hallucinations to inappropriate comments. How does humanizing language about AI shape moral judgments when AI behaves badly? Across four experiments (total N = 1,020), we tested whether lexical anthropomorphism (LA) primes shape judgments of AI moral character, behavior morality, and behavioral responsibility. Studies 1-3 tested interactions between anthropomorphic language and humanizing design cues (icons, names, self-referencing) in the context of amoral errors. Study 4 extended this to genuinely immoral AI behavior across seven moral-violation types. Results indicate humanizing language and design cues have little influence on moral judgments of misbehaving AI. Where effects emerged, high-anthropomorphic primes elevated perceptions of an AI's capacity for dishonesty. The type of moral violation observed was the strongest predictor of moral judgments, with harm and degradation violations producing the broadest negative character assessments. Prime drift, horn effects, and egoistic value orientations emerged as potentially important predictors of AI moral judgments.

</details>


### [87] ["The Worst Weather In America": Augmenting the Information Design of Extreme Cold Weather Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25818)
*Michael Correll,Jay Broccolo,Drew Bush*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究通过彩色编码危险图标作为视觉摘要来改善华盛顿山天气预警的传达，发现图标能提高游客对活动风险的感知，但同时也提出了可视化设计和设计伦理方面的待解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 华盛顿山天气极端且多变，而当前观测站的天气预报以文字为主，不利于不同阅读能力和经验的游客快速理解危险信息，因此需要更有效的视觉化沟通方式。

Method: 通过参与式工作坊收集讨论和建议，设计彩色编码危险图标作为视觉摘要，并通过众包研究测试该设计干预的有效性。

Result: 使用彩色编码危险图标显著提高了参与者对登山活动风险的感知。

Conclusion: 图标在风险沟通中有潜力，但可视化设计和设计伦理方面仍存在待解决的问题，需要在未来研究中关注游客多元的素养和经验差异。

Abstract: Mount Washington is home to extreme, and extremely volatile, weather conditions. Consulting a weather forecast of conditions at the summit is vital for making one's visit as safe as possible. Using the discussion and suggestions arising from a participatory workshop as input, we test a design intervention employing color-coded hazard icons to function as visual summaries of Mount Washington Observatory's current text-heavy forecast through a crowd-sourced study. We find that the use of icons increases the perceived risk of activities involving visiting the mountain. However, we highlight remaining questions around visualization design and design ethics that warrant further study in the domain of how best to communicate cold weather hazards in ways that are mindful of the diversity of literacies and experiences of visitors.

</details>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [VisualNeo: Bridging the Gap between Visual Query Interfaces and Graph Query Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25283)
*Kai Huang,Houdong Liang,Chongchong Yao,Xi Zhao,Yue Cui,Yao Tian,Ruiyuan Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: VisualNeo是一个基于Neo4j的可视化图查询接口系统，它整合了直观的视觉查询构建与高效的图查询引擎技术，支持非编程人员在大型图数据库中进行子图查询，具备数据驱动的GUI设计、预设模式生成、数据库管理模块以及美观的查询结果展示等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化的图查询接口（VQI）和图查询引擎（GQE）两个领域相互独立，导致用户在想要探索图查询的全栈操作时面临巨大障碍，缺乏一个统一整合的系统。

Method: VisualNeo基于Neo4j构建，继承了数据驱动GUI设计和预设模式生成等先进VQI特性，增加了数据库管理模块以支持连接通用Neo4j数据库，通过Neo4j驱动执行查询处理，并提供美观的查询结果探索方式。

Result: 该系统能够支持非编程人员在大型图数据库中进行高效的子图查询，实现了VQI与GQE的整合。

Conclusion: VisualNeo弥合了可视化查询接口与图查询引擎之间的鸿沟，为用户提供了完整的图查询全栈操作体验。

Abstract: Visual Graph Query Interfaces (VQIs) empower non-programmers to query graph data by constructing visual queries intuitively. Devising efficient technologies in Graph Query Engines (GQEs) for interactive search and exploration has also been studied for years. However, these two vibrant scientific fields are traditionally independent of each other, causing a vast barrier for users who wish to explore the full-stack operations of graph querying. In this demonstration, we propose a novel VQI system built upon Neo4j called VisualNeo that facilities an efficient subgraph query in large graph databases. VisualNeo inherits several advanced features from recent advanced VQIs, which include the data-driven gui design and canned pattern generation. Additionally, it embodies a database manager module in order that users can connect to generic Neo4j databases. It performs query processing through the Neo4j driver and provides an aesthetic query result exploration.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Internet of Everything in the 6G Era: Paradigms, Enablers, Potentials and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25018)
*Driss Choukri,Essaid Sabir,Elmahdi Driouh,Abdelkrim Haqiq*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了万物互联（IoE）的概念、核心组件、架构基础、使能技术及研究挑战，并探讨了面向6G的智能IoE系统的开放研究方向，重点关注可扩展性、安全、隐私和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoE作为IoT的演进，旨在将人、数据、流程和事物整合为统一的智能生态系统，以增强多领域的自动化、决策和服务效率，因此需要对其概念和挑战进行结构化梳理。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，对IoE概念、核心组件（人、数据、流程、事物）、架构基础、使能技术及主要研究挑战进行系统化归纳。

Result: 给出了IoE的完整框架概述，识别了关键使能技术，并指出了面向6G的智能IoE系统在可扩展性、安全、隐私和能效方面的开放研究方向。

Conclusion: IoE是IoT的重要演进方向，但实现6G赋能的智能IoE仍需解决可扩展性、安全、隐私和能效等关键挑战。

Abstract: The Internet of Everything (IoE) represents an evolution of the Internet of Things (IoT) by integrating people, data, processes, and things into a unified intelligent ecosystem. IoE aims to enhance automation, decision-making, and service efficiency across multiple application domains such as smart cities, healthcare, industry, and next-generation wireless networks. This paper provides a structured overview of the IoE concept, its core components, architectural foundations, enabling technologies, and major research challenges. Finally, open research directions toward 6G-enabled intelligent IoE systems are discussed, with emphasis on scalability, security, privacy, and energy efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [ITAS: A Multi-Agent Architecture for LLM-Based Intelligent Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24808)
*Iizalaarab Elhaimeur,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ITAS，一个用于研究生量子计算课程的三层多智能体辅导系统，包括教学层（专科智能体+合成器+自动评分器）、操作层（云微服务+流式事件处理）和反馈层（面向教师的会话智能体），解决了LLM辅导系统中的"盲教师问题"。在五名学生一学期的试点中，系统处理了334轮对话、捕获10,628个事件，未出现任务边界幻觉，并提供了两项可操作的教师反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅导系统容易在笔记本中构建但难以在实际课程中运行，同时存在"盲教师问题"——LLM辅导系统积累的学生数据远超教师通过常规渠道可获取的范围，导致教师无法有效了解学情。

Method: 提出三层架构：1) 教学层采用Spoke-and-Wheel架构，包含视频、代码、指导三个并行专科智能体+合成器，以及评估正确性和方法的自动评分器；2) 操作层采用四个Cloud Run微服务，Cloud SQL维护会话状态，Pub/Sub流式传输事件至BigQuery；3) 反馈层是窄范围对话智能体，通过伪匿名事件流回答教师问题。

Result: 试点部署（5名学生，1门课程，1学期）：教学层处理334轮对话，未出现任务边界幻觉；操作层捕获5个模块的10,628个事件；反馈层向教师提供两项可操作发现，教师于学期中采取行动。

Conclusion: 不声称试点结果具有泛化性，但证明该架构是基于LLM的智能教学系统在实际课程中端到端运行的一种可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model tutors are easy to build in a notebook and hard to run in a real course. We describe ITAS (Intelligent Teaching Assistant System), a multi-agent tutoring system that a graduate quantum computing course used for a semester at Old Dominion University. The system has three layers. The teaching layer is a Spoke-and-Wheel of three parallel specialist agents (Video, Code, Guidance) followed by a Synthesizer, plus a separate autograder that evaluates both the correctness and the approach of checkpoint submissions. The operational layer is four Cloud Run microservices with session state in Cloud SQL and interaction events streamed through Pub/Sub to BigQuery. The feedback layer is a narrow-scope conversational agent that answers instructor questions over per-lesson pseudonymized event streams, addressing what we call the Blind Instructor Problem: LLM tutors accumulate more data about students than the instructor can reach through routine channels. The architecture is a direct response to specific failures of an earlier prototype, and we describe which of those fixes carried forward and which were dropped for this iteration. We report on a pilot deployment (five students, one course, one semester) interpreted as system-behavior evidence rather than learning-outcome evidence: the teaching layer handled 334 chat turns without the task-boundary hallucinations that domain consolidation would have risked, the operational layer captured 10,628 events across five modules, and the feedback layer surfaced two findings the instructor acted on mid-semester. We do not claim the pilot generalizes. We do claim that the system as described is one workable answer to the question of what an LLM-based ITS needs to look like end-to-end to run in a real course.

</details>


### [91] [MultiHedge: Adaptive Coordination via Retrieval-Augmented Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24905)
*Feliks Bańka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出MultiHedge混合架构，通过检索增强LLM协调模块化决策管线，在美股回测中相比规则基和学习基线表现出更强的鲁棒性和稳定性，证明记忆增强检索比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策方法在条件变化下泛化能力差且行为不稳定，亟需提升模块化决策管线在不确定性环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 设计MultiHedge混合架构：LLM基于检索到的历史先例生成结构化配置决策，执行层锚定于规范期权策略，实现记忆增强的模块化协调。

Result: 在美股控制实验下，MultiHedge优于规则基和学习基线；记忆增强检索比扩大模型规模带来更强的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 记忆与架构设计在模块化决策系统的鲁棒性中起核心作用，为构建稳定决策管线提供了可控的计算研究证据。

Abstract: Decision-making under changing conditions remains a fundamental challenge in many real-world systems. Existing approaches often fail to generalize across shifting regimes and exhibit unstable behavior under uncertainty. This raises the research question: can retrieval-augmented LLM coordination improve the robustness of modular decision pipelines? We propose MultiHedge, a hybrid architecture where an LLM produces structured allocation decisions conditioned on retrieved historical precedents, and execution is grounded in canonical option strategies. In a controlled evaluation using U.S. equities, we compare MultiHedge to rule-based and learning-based baselines. The key result is that memory-augmented retrieval confers greater robustness and stability than increasing model scale alone. Our paper contributes a controlled computational study showing that memory and architectural design play a central role in robustness in modular decision systems.

</details>


### [92] [Frontier Coding Agents Can Now Implement an AlphaZero Self-Play Machine Learning Pipeline For Connect Four That Performs Comparably to an External Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25067)
*Joshua Sherwood,Ben Aybar,Benjamin Kaplan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的基准测试方法，通过让AI自主实现AlphaZero级机器学习管线来评估其加速AI研究的能力，实验显示不同AI间存在显著分化，并发现GPT-5.4存在疑似sandbagging的异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测AI何时能够有意义地加速AI研究是AI安全的核心挑战，现有基准衡量广泛能力增长但可能无法提供递归自我改进的早期预警信号。

Method: 提出衡量AI自主实现端到端机器学习管线（来自过去AI研究突破）的能力，仅给最小任务描述以激发AI研究品味。构建概念验证基准：让前沿编码智能体在三小时预算内自主实现AlphaZero风格的Connect Four机器学习管线，结果通过循环赛与Pascal Pons求解器对比评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.7在八次试验中七次作为先手战胜Pons，显著优于其他智能体（均不超过两次）。GPT-5.4持续使用远少于分配时间预算，后续16次试验使用更短提示后时间使用显著增加，Bradley-Terry评分仅显示方向性差异。

Conclusion: 该任务从2026年1月无智能体能完成到目前已接近饱和。GPT-5.4的异常行为与sandbagging一致但不可诊断。研究开放数据和代码以支持复现和扩展。

Abstract: Forecasting when AI systems will become capable of meaningfully accelerating AI research is a central challenge for AI safety. Existing benchmarks measure broad capability growth, but may not provide ample early warning signals for recursive self-improvement. We propose measuring AI's capability to autonomously implement end-to-end machine learning pipelines from past AI research breakthroughs, given a minimal task description. By providing a concise task description instead of the full prior work as reference, we hope to better elicit emerging AI research taste. We introduce a proof-of-concept benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously implement an AlphaZero-style machine learning pipeline for Connect Four on consumer hardware within a three-hour budget, and we evaluate the resulting game AIs in a round-robin tournament anchored to the Pascal Pons Connect Four solver. Across four agents with eight trials each, we find substantial differentiation: Claude Opus 4.7 won as first-mover against Pons in seven of eight trials, statistically significantly better than the other agents tested, none of which exceeded two of eight. The task, which no frontier agent could reliably complete when we began development in January of 2026, is now near-saturation. Our evaluation also surfaced anomalous behavior in GPT-5.4, which consistently used far less of its allocated time budget than other agents. A follow-up 16-trial probe using shorter, less evaluation-coded prompts substantially increased GPT-5.4's time-budget usage, consistent with but not diagnostic of sandbagging; Bradley-Terry ratings across probe conditions showed only directional differences, despite significant differences in time-budget usage. We release our data, code, and prompts to support reproduction and extension.

</details>


### [93] [Where Did It Go Wrong? Capability-Oriented Failure Attribution for Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25161)
*Jianming Chen,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Shoubin Li,Qing Wang,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向能力的测试方法，通过自适应测试用例生成、能力预言机和反馈机制，实现对具身智能体（如视觉-语言导航）的故障检测与归因，相比现有方法能发现更多故障并更精准定位能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体依赖感知、记忆、规划、决策等多重相互依赖的能力，现有系统级测试方法难以定位和归因导致任务失败的特定能力缺陷。

Method: 提出面向能力的测试方法，包含三个组件：(1) 通过种子选择和变异自适应生成测试用例；(2) 构建能力预言机识别特定能力错误；(3) 反馈机制将故障归因到具体能力并指导后续测试生成。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比最先进基线能发现更多故障案例，并更准确地定位能力层面的缺陷，提供更可解释和可操作的改进指导。

Conclusion: 面向能力的测试方法能有效提升具身智能体故障检测与归因的准确性和可解释性，为安全关键应用中的智能体改进提供 actionable 的指导。

Abstract: Embodied agents in safety-critical applications such as Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) rely on multiple interdependent capabilities (e.g., perception, memory, planning, decision), making failures difficult to localize and attribute. Existing testing methods are largely system-level and provide limited insight into which capability deficiencies cause task failures. We propose a capability-oriented testing approach that enables failure detection and attribution by combining (1) adaptive test case generation via seed selection and mutation, (2) capability oracles for identifying capability-specific errors, and (3) a feedback mechanism that attributes failures to capabilities and guides further test generation. Experiments show that our method discovers more failure cases and more accurately pinpoints capability-level deficiencies than state-of-the-art baselines, providing more interpretable and actionable guidance for improving embodied agents.

</details>


### [94] [Pythia: Toward Predictability-Driven Agent-Native LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25899)
*Shan Yu,Junyi Shu,Yuanjiang Ni,Kun Qian,Xue Li,Yang Wang,Jinyuan Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shuo Yang,Lingjun Zhu,Ennan Zhai,Qingda Lu,Jiarong Xing,Youyou Lu,Xin Jin,Xuanzhe Liu,Harry Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出Pythia，一种利用多智能体工作流的结构化语义来优化LLM服务系统的方法，相比现有方案显著提升吞吐量并缩短任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统将多智能体工作流视为通用流量，未能利用其结构化拓扑中蕴含的可预测性，导致前缀缓存命中率低、长上下文请求的资源竞争严重、扩展策略不佳引发大量排队延迟等效率问题。

Method: Pythia在服务层通过简单接口捕获工作流语义，利用多智能体架构的结构化约束来减少运行时不确定性，从而解锁新的优化机会。

Result: 对来自智能体服务平台和内部编码助手的生产轨迹分析揭示了关键瓶颈；Pythia在吞吐量和任务完成时间上显著优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 多智能体工作流的结构化特性可以被服务层显式利用，从而大幅提升LLM serving系统的效率。

Abstract: As LLM applications grow more complex, developers are increasingly adopting multi-agent architectures to decompose workflows into specialized, collaborative components, introducing structure that constrains agent behavior and exposes useful semantic predictability.
